`func_ov000_02171478` (3072 bytes, a 22-case message handler that creates task objects with `new`) was drafted for the first time this round (`scratchpad/big9/1478/best22.cpp`, 22 aligned diffs). Each case is `if (a != 0) return; if (Try(task, b, c)) return; self->vfunc_08(a, kind, b, c);`, where `Try` is an existing out-of-line function or an inlined `BOOL` helper. The helpers' return forms decide the code:

- **Two nested `? TRUE : FALSE` inlines give two normalisations.** The target tests one field with `movne r0, #1; moveq r0, #0; cmp r0, #0` twice in a row. `return Inner(q) ? TRUE : FALSE;` around `static inline BOOL Inner(q) { return q->f != NULL ? TRUE : FALSE; }` reproduces it; one inline gives one.
- **Early `return FALSE` lets MWCC predicate a bitfield set.** `if (p == NULL) return FALSE; p->b0 = 1; return TRUE;` gave the target's `ldrne/bicne/orrne/strne` with `moveq r2, #0` / `movne r2, #1`. Writing it as `if (p != NULL) {...; return TRUE;} return FALSE;` or with a result variable gave a branch around the store (16 diffs).
- **A test whose answer is always TRUE stays a test.** In one case the target sets the result to 1 on both paths (`moveq r2, sl` and `movne r2, sl`) and still emits the base call after `cmp r2, #0`. `if (p == NULL) return TRUE; p->b0 = 1; return TRUE;` keeps it. `if (p != NULL) p->b0 = 1; return TRUE;` folds the test and drops the 14-instruction base call.
- **Assignment order of byte and halfword locals sets their spill slots.** Values computed before an allocation and passed to the constructor afterwards go to `fp` or the stack. Swapping two assignments (`lo = b; hi = (b >> 8) & 0xffffff;`) moved them to the target's homes. Declaration order made no difference here (`permblk.py` over the declarations).
- **Load order of a ring buffer's indices.** The inlined `MessageQueue_FindById` in the target reads `writeIndex` before `readIndex`. A local copy of the inline that reads `end` first fixed the load order. The header version reads `readIndex` first.
- **Still open in `02171478`.** In the `FindById` loop the target colours `q, i, index, result` as `r3, r2, r1, r0`. The draft has `r0, r3, r2, r1`. Writing `index` as a ternary (`u32 index = i >= 16 ? i - 16 : i;`, draft `alt14_regs.cpp`, 14 diffs) gives the target's registers for `q` and the result, but `sub; cmp; movlo` instead of `mov; cmp; subhs`. The type scan, declaration order, a `Mgr *` parameter, a result variable, `self->messages + index` and four ways of writing the caller changed nothing. Four more diffs are scheduling in the `0x3c` allocation case.
- **`tools/permuter/cc.sh` writes to `/tmp`** (`mktemp -p /tmp`). Don't use it in sessions that must not write outside the clone and scratch directory.
- **`func_ov000_02166be8` (29, registers of the 64-bit tail) did not move.** `automover.sh` ran over 25 of the 44 statements of `best29.cpp`. The only lower scores came from moving the case 4 call or another case's statement into the tail, which changes the code. A tail variable separate from `e`, reusing `tgt`, no variable at all (re-reading `w->cb4`), `dx`/`dz`/`ox` in a block in all six declaration orders, `sq` as an inline (two forms) and no `sq` variable all gave the same 29. MWCC splits a variable into separate live ranges, so which variable holds a value does not matter there.
- **`func_ov007_021ce038` queue scans.** Reading `write` from the global in the loop condition (`i != ((Ov007MsgQ *)data_ov000_021ccd54)->write`) took the draft from 84 to 58 aligned (25 structural, `scratchpad/big9/ce038/f3.cpp`), but the target loads `read` and `write` once. The target also reloads the queue pointer before each scan. Locals for `read`/`write` with the global read in the loop, a queue parameter and a `volatile` cast on the global all loaded the global only once.
## Inline return types and the extended type scan (all overlays, round ovhi16)

- **Declare a count inline as returning `long`.** `func_ov010_0220dbc0` (10 diffs) and `func_ov010_0220a374` (32) both loop `for (i = 0; i < Count(coll); i++)`, where `Count` is `u8 r = 0; if (c->b0) r = c->count; return r;`. The draft had the loop index and the looked-up id (or object) in each other's callee-saved registers. Declaration order, block-scoped locals, a `while` loop and every type for `i` and `id` changed nothing (an `s16 i` fixed the registers but added a sign extension to `i++`). Changing only the inline's return type to `long` (`static inline long Count(...)`, same body) matched both. `s32`, `int` and `u32` did not. As with the round ovhi15 `long` locals, `long` and `int` are both 32 bits, but the implicit conversion at the comparison changes the allocation order.
- **A `BOOL` inline declared as `s16`.** `func_ov000_0218af9c` (2 diffs since round lo13: the spill store of `on` came after the `lsl`) matched when the early-return "game idle" inline `GIdle` (`if (Busy(g)) return FALSE; if (g->state != 3) return FALSE; return TRUE;`) was declared `static inline s16` instead of `BOOL`. None of the 720 statement orders, the declaration climb or the permuter had found it.
- **`s32` for a local that holds an `s16` field.** In `func_ov003_021d7e58` (ticker interpolation, 7 diffs) `cur = self->cur; v = cur + (s16)FxMul(self->next - cur, 0x1000 / d);` matched once `cur` was `s32` instead of `s16`.
- **The type scan now covers inline signatures.** `scratchpad/ovhi16/t/ts.py` (from round ovhi15) also tries the return type and the scalar parameters of every inline the function uses, and tries `s16`/`u16` on 32-bit locals. All four matches above came from it. Across 100 drafts below 0x500 bytes in every overlay, it also improved `func_ov002_021e0280` (20 to 9, `long i`), `func_ov002_021dedf0` (20 to 9, `s16 w` in the inline), `func_ov003_021d6a3c` (36 to 16, a `long` inline parameter), `func_ov000_021597dc` (34 to 18, `u8 trig`), `func_ov002_021d8d00` (34 to 24) and `func_ov010_0220dbc0`'s twin above. It costs a few seconds per draft, so run it on every new near-miss. Any result that narrows a type (`s16`, `u8`) must be checked by hand for truncation.
- **`automover.sh` found nothing.** It ran on 28 drafts from ov000 low and overlays 2–15. Its few lower scores either changed what the code does (`func_ov002_021e5974`: a store moved above the loop that sets its value) or traded a register swap for a store-order swap (`func_ov003_021ddce4`, `func_ov002_021e2690`).
- **A matching draft that cannot be split: `func_ov000_0214b670`.** The draft `scratchpad/ov000q/w/b654.cpp` matches, but after it splits out, the gap from `0x0214b68c` to `0x0214b6d0` holds only `func_ov000_0214b68c`. Nothing references that function: its two `bl`s go to TWL-only code (`relocs.txt` has `module:none` for `0x02224e44` and `0x02224eb8`). The linker's `-dead` then strips the gap, and every later function in ov000 shifts. Before `0214b670` was split out, the callers of `0214b670` in ov003 and ov011 kept the gap alive. `func_ov000_0214b438`..`0214b5bc` and `0214b724`/`0214b810` also call TWL-only code and have no drafts.
- **Still open, with this round's attempts.** `func_ov003_021ddce4` (3 diffs; draft `scratchpad/ovhi16/w/dce.c`): MWCC hoists the shared first `bic #1` of both branches out of the `if`, and the target does not hoist it. Swapping `b1 = 0` and `b0 = 0` in the zero branch gives 2 diffs, but then the `biceq`s are in the wrong order. `-1`/`TRUE`/a variable for the 1, a parameterised inline, `!= 0` with swapped branches and pointer-expression variants did not help. `func_ov010_02201bf4` (4): the volatile flag byte must stay in `r1` and the shifted copy go to `r2`. Taking the byte's address (as `func_ov010_02201ad4` does) gives exactly that allocation, but it leaves a stack frame in this leaf function. `func_ov000_0217a1f8` (3): the target materialises `-1` with `mvn` where MWCC derives it from the `0x78` argument (`sub r2, r1, #0x79`), whatever the parameter types are. Its sibling `func_ov000_0217a480` does the same `sub` in the target. `func_ov002_021e2690` (4): two loop-invariant constants (3 and 8) are hoisted into each other's registers. Swapping their stores fixes the registers but not the store order. `func_ov003_021e1204`, `func_ov015_02211b2c`, `func_ov006_021e51f8` and `func_ov006_021e9f80` (2 each) are register and scheduling swaps that none of the scans moved.

## Per-scan reloads of a global, and what the type scan did on big functions (all overlays, round big10)

- **A global that the target reloads before every inlined scan: read it through a `volatile u32` lvalue.** In `func_ov007_021ce038` case 5, four inlined ring-buffer scans test four ids. The target loads `read`/`write` once into `r0`/`r1` and keeps them, but reloads the queue pointer (`ldr rX, =data_ov000_021ccd54; ldr r5, [rX]`) before scans 2–4. With `read`/`write` as caller locals passed to the inline, MWCC kept the pointer in a register for all four scans, whether the inline read the global in its header, in the loop or through a pointer parameter. `Ov007MsgQ *q = (Ov007MsgQ *)*(volatile u32 *)&data_ov000_021ccd54;` in the inline header gave the reloads. The form `*(Ov007MsgQ * volatile *)&data_ov000_021ccd54` (a volatile pointer type in the cast) was ignored: the load was still shared. Writing the loop test as `w != i` instead of `i != w` then fixed the registers of the scans (58 to 44 aligned), and the type scan added `long w` for the caller's `write` local (42, `scratchpad/big10/best/ce038_42.cpp`). Still open there: the first scan should reuse the pointer the caller loaded `read`/`write` through (passing that pointer to the first call only gives this, but swaps two register pairs, 55), the `Ov007Cam_SetA` load order, and `FLD(u16, m, 0xc2) &= ~0x20` (pool `0xffdf`) against the target's `bic #0x20`. A temporary (`u32 t = FLD(u16, m, 0xc2); FLD(u16, m, 0xc2) = t & ~0x20;`) gives the `bic`, but like the 1-bit field it recolours the whole function (452 aligned, 14 structural).
- **`long` on a loop bound in a ring-buffer lookup.** In `func_ov000_02171478` (`alt14_regs.cpp`, the ternary-index draft) `long end` in the inlined `FindById` put `i` in the target's register (14 to 11, `scratchpad/big10/best/1478_11.cpp`). `end` and `index` are still swapped (`sl`/`r1`), and every form that gives that allocation (ternary, `if`/`else`, a wrapper inline returning early) compiles to `sub; cmp; movlo` where the target has `mov; cmp; subhs`. The `index = i; if (index >= 16) index = i - 16;` form gives the target's instructions with `long end` too, but the old register rotation (23).
- **The extended type scan on the big near-misses.** `ts.py` from round ovhi16 ran on all eleven drafts. It improved `func_ov000_021b2f08` (85 to 80, `long j`), the new `021ce038` draft above (44 to 42) and `func_ov011_021ff24c` (83 to 76 with `long` for the `F24c_RingSize` inline, `s16 found`, and `s32` for the ring index local and the `F24c_Idx` parameter; `scratchpad/big10/best/f24c_76.cpp`). Its other finds narrowed types against the target: `u8` for a busy-node count (66) adds an `and #0xff` the target does not have, and `u8 ox` in `func_ov000_02166be8` (29 to 28) truncates a 32-bit offset, so neither was kept. Nothing changed for `02204428`, `021ad8ac`, `021fc0b8`, `02171478` (header-form draft), `021fe2a4`, `021d112c` or `021fb1b4`.
- **`e == 2 || e == 3` always became a range check in `021ff24c`.** The target tests `cmp #2; beq; cmp #3; movne`. Reversed operands (`e == 3 || e == 2`), nested ternaries, `|` of two compares, a result variable set in nested `if`s, two one-compare inlines, early returns, a `switch` and a pointer parameter (`*p == 2 || *p == 3`) all gave `sub #2; cmp #1` or worse.
- **Smaller notes.** In `func_ov010_021fe2a4` the target's zero lives in `r6` and `flag` is a copy of it (`mov sb, r6` at the switch head), with the loop index copied from `flag` inside case `0x14`. Ten ways of initialising `flag` and `i` (separate statements, chained assignment, `i = flag`, a `zero` local) gave either the old 22 or a single `cmp` at the switch head. In `func_ov011_021fc0b8` the target materialises the `1` passed to two calls (`on`) early in `sl`, while the draft materialises the `0` stored by the inlined list removal there. Store order in the removal, a literal `TRUE`, `on` at other scopes and a typed key parameter changed nothing.

## TU2 string-pool blockers, round 3 (round mainpool4)

`func_0205b68c` matched, so 8 TU2 blockers are left and TU2 is still not merged. Drafts are in `scratchpad/mainpool4/d/` (and `a358/`, `b18/`, `e5/`).

- **Reuse the bit inlines of the sibling files.** `func_0205b68c` (StageRes variable get/set, now at the end of `StageRes_4.cpp`) was stuck at 41 with hand-written bit loops. Its get case is `DataRes_ReadBits` (the inlined `func_02059454` that `func_0205b588` in the same file already uses): 41 to 18. The set case is the same loop shape as `DataRes_WriteBitsAt` in `DataRes_4.cpp`, with `bytes[byte] &= ~(1 << sh); bytes[byte] |= v << sh;` as two statements: 18 to 10. The last 10 were a swap of `bytes` and `i`, fixed by declaring `u8 *bytes;` before `u16 i;` in the write inline. Before you hand-write a loop, look for an inline or a matched function in the neighbouring files that does the same thing.
- **Inline parameters in reverse order set the argument load order.** In `func_02060a08` the target loads `args[3]`, `args[2]`, `args[1]` in that order, the draft in the reverse order. MWCC evaluates inline arguments right to left, so the key bytes are the inline's last parameters: `Flags_AddSlot(StageFlags *, u8 d, u8 c, u8 b, u8 a)` called as `(args[0], args[1], args[2], args[3])`. With the key written `(d & 0xff) | (((u32)(b << 24) >> 8) | (a << 24) | ((u32)(c << 24) >> 16))` (the `b` term first, so the `>> 8` is folded into the `orr` and `a << 24` stays in a register) the function is register-only: 0 masked sequence diffs, 40 aligned (`d/0a08_m3.cpp`). The permuter then found that a function-scope `StageFlags *f;`, assigned `f = data_0214a424;` in each branch and passed to the inline, takes 40 to 17 (`d/0a08_s3.c`). A block-scoped `StageFlags *f = data_0214a424;` gives 40 again. The remaining 17 are the first branch's registers. Loop-index types, a `p == NULL` early return, a `u16 v = args[1]` local (29) and copies of `args` changed nothing. The same reversal does nothing for `func_02060b18` (21, `b18/min_c.c`); there declaring `u32 i;` before `u32 key` made it worse (34).
- **`func_0205a358` written from scratch (ObjPool update, 0 masked, 71 aligned).** It walks the pool list. Entries whose colour differs from `data_02fe1534` are removed with `func_020599f4`. Otherwise, if the player exists and is free and `active & 1` is set, `kind` 1, 2 and 5 call `func_0205a028`, a box test and `func_0205a268`. Any of these that fires returns, and if none does, the entry's `active &= ~4`. The box test is an inline like `func_0205a028`. It uses `ObjPool_InBox(e, dx, dy, dz)` (abs of each delta, then `dx < unk_18 && dy < unk_1c && dz < unk_20`), with `dx`, `dy`, `dz` declared as locals in x, y, z order in both branches. Passing the subtractions as arguments gives `ldm` and the wrong load order, and a vector-pointer form gives 98. `func_ov000_0218c040`'s kind parameter must be `int` (the target does `asr #12` on the `u16` with no truncation). `func_0205a028` still matches with that prototype. The whole function is one register permutation: the target has `pool` in `r4` and the entry in `r5`, and the loop invariants in `lr`/`r6`-`r8` with `i` in `ip`. The draft has them the other way round. Declaration order, `for`/`while`/`continue` forms, the colour and player checks or the switch as inlines, the loop as a pointer, `while` or `break` form, `ts.py` and `automover.sh` changed nothing (`d/a358_c.cpp`).
- **`func_0205e29c` (12 aligned).** The comma expression `(0, (StageArea *)...)` in the old draft can be replaced by an inline `GetArea(slot, slot->args[2])`, which gives the same code (`d/e29c_g.cpp`). Without either, it is 26. The register swap at the top also decides which register holds the NULL result of `Stage_FindObj3D` (the target reuses the zero of `unk_80 = 0`), and that moves the `unk_14` store and the pool order. `ts.py`, `automover.sh`, moving `unk_80 = 0`, and `if`/ternary/early-return forms of the 3D lookup changed nothing.
- **`func_0205e5c4` (22 to 19 aligned, `d/e5c4_a.cpp`).** Computing `(u32)(type << 28)` after the clear loop (not into a local before it) fixes the order. What is left is one extra callee-saved register (`sb`): the draft keeps `type` in `r8` because `lr` holds `0xffff` while `type` is live. A constructor for the parameter block runs at the declaration (before the `Field_HasEvent` test) and costs 103. `ts.py` changed nothing.
- **`func_0205c234` (2) and `func_0205b1b4` (2).** Neither changed. For `c234`, 20 minutes of the permuter on the C draft (`d/c234v3.c`, about 3000 iterations), `rso.py`, `ts.py`, `automover.sh`, a `Slot_Set` inline with parameters (4), loops for `args[2..5]` and chained assignments all failed. The header `Slot_Alloc()`/`StageRes_Last()` forms reload `slotEnd` after the slot stores (41 and 48). For `b1b4`, all 120 orders of the five stores keep 2. Pointer locals, a zero local, chained assignment and a `Clr(&data_02149efc)` inline did too.
- **`tdis.py`** (`scratchpad/mainpool4/tdis.py addr`) prints a main function's target code from the base ROM with relocation targets. `match.py` only shows as many target lines as the draft has.
## Shifting coordinates in place, and the joint move/type climb (all overlays, round ovhi17)

- **Copy a position into locals first, then shift or offset the locals in place.** Three screen-position functions matched once the coordinate arithmetic was split into a plain copy followed by in-place updates. Writing the arithmetic in one expression gives the same instructions but puts the intermediate values in other registers, or schedules the loads in another order.
  - `func_ov000_021c2e64` (7 diffs): the inline that calls `o->vfunc_20((x + 0x8000) >> 12, (y + ((h - o->unk48) << 11)) >> 12)` matched once its by-value parameters were updated first: `x += 0x8000; y += (h - o->unk48) << 11; o->vfunc_20(x >> 12, y >> 12);`.
  - `func_ov000_021c28cc` (6): `x = ((self->x + 0xa000) >> 12) - 0x80; y = ((((self->h - 8) << 12) + self->y) >> 12) - 0x60;` became an inline with out-parameters, `ScreenPos(&x, &y, self->x, self->y, self->h)`, whose body is `x += 0xa000; y += (h - 8) << 12; *ox = (x >> 12) - 0x80; *oy = (y >> 12) - 0x60;`. Taking the address of the two locals does not force them to the stack once the inline is expanded.
  - `func_ov006_021df5a0` (11): in the loop, `x = out.x >> 12; y = base + (i * 9 + (out.y >> 12) + 8);` became `x = out.x; y = out.y; x >>= 12; y = base + (i * 9 + (y >> 12) + 8);`.
  In each case the original draft was already right except for the registers or the order of the two coordinate loads. Try this split whenever a near-miss computes `(a.x OP k) >> 12` and `(a.y OP k) >> 12` in one statement. It also brought the Caption draw code (`func_ov006_021e0b80`, from 23 to 8 diffs; the same code inlined in `func_ov006_021e1bb4`, from 16 to 5) closer: `ox = self->unk10 + 0x80; oy = self->unk14 + 0x60; x = ...; y = ...; ox += (w - a44) >> 1; oy += (h - a48) >> 1;`. In both, the `y` half still lands in the wrong registers. The final `oy` is computed into the register of `h - a48`, not in place. No variable split tried so far reproduces that.
- **Case order decides how identical `switch` arms are merged.** `func_ov002_021e6508` has a rate inline that is `switch (mode) { case 0: return r; case 1: return r; default: return r; }`. Written in that order, each arm keeps its own `b`. Listing `case 1` before `case 0` gives the target's `beq L; cmp #1; b L; L:` (7 diffs down to 2). The remaining 2 diffs are the loads of `0x7c` and `0x88` in swapped order. Return-type, local and `FX_MUL` operand-order variants did not swap them. Draft: `scratchpad/ovhi17/d/ov002_021e6508/s0.cpp`.
- **`u8 n` is the right type for `func_ov003_021e6274`, but it moves `self`.** The target masks the stage number with `and #0xff` (from 14 diffs, the remaining ones are exactly that). Declaring `n` as `u8` fixes every mask but swaps `self` and the part pointer between `r6` and `r7` (46 diffs). The `Ov003Obj *me = self` trick that fixed those registers for `u16 n` no longer helps. The joint climb below brings the `u8` draft down to 6, but only by moving `n = n - 1;` above the `n == self->unk_93_1` test, which changes behaviour. Draft: `scratchpad/ovhi17/d/ov003_021e6274/s1.cpp`.
- **The joint move/type climb (`scratchpad/ovhi17/t/combo2.py`) found nothing new.** It scores every single-line move (within `MAXD` lines) and every type change from `ts.py` together, deduplicates the neighbours by the hash of the compiled code, and looks two steps ahead from the best different-code neighbours. On `func_ov002_021e0280`, `021dedf0`, `021e6508`, `021d6a3c`, `021e1bb4`, `021e0b80`, `0220640c` and `func_ov003_021e6274` it either found nothing or only moved a call (`func_020627dc(4)` in `021e6274`, 14 to 13, which changes the order of side effects). Hashing the compiled code matters: about 95% of the moves in `021e0280` (1049 neighbours) compile to the current code, so a lookahead that does not skip them wastes its budget on declaration moves.
- **Still open from this round.** `func_ov003_021e1204` (2): the `u16 k = (u16)(e->unk_16 >> 12)` load goes to `r1` in the target and to `r0` in ours, whatever the inline or `switch` form. `func_ov003_021ead50` (2): the target emits `cmp; strne; beq` from one test. Two separate `if (t != NULL)` tests give that shape plus a second `cmp`. `func_ov013_022001b8` (3): `&&` chains with a constant first term, inline predicates and if/else locals all give `mov #1; movne #0`. `func_ov006_021d5b98`/`021d5e68` (2 each): one `ldr r1, =str` is scheduled after `ldr r0, [r5]`. Neither real string literals nor other `extern` types for the strings change it. The strings `0x021ebfe0..0x021ec035` are one pool shared with `021d6140`, `021d6724` and `__sinit_ov006_021ebadc`. `func_ov009_02206084` (5): the target loads `pl->id` before the bit-field store. Storing `unk_90` first gets the order right but moves `&p` into `r1` too early (14 diffs). `func_ov010_0220640c` (5): `FLD(u16, self, 0x98) += (u16)(... + 0x7fff)` fixes the load order (draft `d/ov010_0220640c/s1.cpp`), but `mov r2, #0` for the next call is still scheduled late.

## TU1 blockers, round 3 (main, round mainpool6)

- **An integer add keeps a member offset from being folded into the field offset: `func_02050ebc` matched** (now at the end of `TextWin_11.cpp`, whose `delinks.txt` entry grows to `0x02050f70`). The target reads the owner's id as `add r0, r4, #0x3c0; ldrh r0, [r0, #0x12]`. Every member, cast, base-class and inline form folds `0x3c0 + 0x12` into `0x300 + 0xd2` (rounds mainpool3 and mainpool5). `((NameOwnerSub *)((s32)owner + 0x3c0))->id` keeps the two parts apart and matches. `(u32)owner + 0x3c0` and `(u8 *)owner + 0x3c0` still fold; only the signed integer cast works. When a large field offset is split at a sub-object boundary instead of at `& ~0xff`, try computing the sub-object address as an `s32` sum.
- **How MWCC orders callee-saved registers, measured on small test functions** (`scratchpad/mainpool6/x/t*.c`, disassembled with `t/odis.py`). Locals get registers in declaration order, so the first-declared local gets the lowest register. Hoisted loop-invariant constants (`mov rX, #imm` in the preheader) get lower registers than every local, and the later-materialized constant gets the lowest. Hoisted non-constant expressions (`n * 3` with a parameter `n`) and parameters get registers above the locals. The target's VRAM-free loop (`0204f524`, `0204f5bc`, `02051210`, `0205592c`) has `i` below both constants (`i` r5, `0x6400000` r6, `0x6600000` r7), which is what we get when the bases are parameters or other non-constant invariants, not constants. `func_02051a34` (the hoisted `0xffff0000` above `win` and `fn`) and `func_0204ffdc` (the zero register above the line pointer, see below) show the same pattern. So in all three the target's constant behaves like a variable or an expression that became constant after hoisting. Nothing found so far does this in a draft. These all still give constant ordering: locals initialised at the top (also `register`, `void *`, `u8 *`, self-assignments, assignment in both arms of an `if`, assignment via `Set(&lo, ...)`), aggregate or array initialisers (these go to the stack), every compiler version under `tools/mwccarm`, `-O4,s`/`-O3`, and the pragmas `opt_strength_reduction`, `opt_lifetimes`, `opt_propagation`, `opt_dead_assignments`, `opt_dead_code`, `opt_unroll_loops`, `optimization_level 1`..`3` and `ipa file`. A struct returned by value from an inline (`VB v = MkVB();`) does keep the bases as variables, but adds a stack temporary. A second use of the constants before or after the loop does not move them to the entry block of `02051210`, and neither do `delete` for the `obj` release or `do`/`while` loop forms. Loop-index types change nothing either.
- **`func_0204ffdc` (52).** The type scan (`ts.py`, 19 declarations) found nothing. Inserting `dy = 0;`, `dy += 0;` or `dy = dy;` at every statement slot (`t/ins.py`) changed nothing either. In our build the add is `add r2, r0, #0`, so the zero reaches the backend as an immediate. In the target it is `add r2, sb, r0`, a register that holds 0.
- **`func_020508f4` (8).** 50 minutes of the permuter (about 2500 iterations, standalone C++ draft `d/08f4p.cpp` without the `TextWin` header), `automover.sh` over every statement, an inline for the copy loop (with parameters in both orders, or a `char **` parameter), calling `font->cbCharSpliter` directly (91) and removing or inlining `tag` all left the `fn`/`dst` swap.
## Expanded compound assignments, reusing a setter inline, and what the newer main-module tricks did on big overlay functions (all overlays, round big11)

Nothing matched this round. `func_ov007_021ce038` went from 42 to 19 sequence diffs (`scratchpad/big11/best/ce038_19.cpp`; `ce038_20.cpp` is the same without the extra scan inline).

- **`x = x & ~m` is not `x &= ~m` on a `u16` field.** `FLD(u16, m, 0xc2) &= ~0x20` (in the inlined `Ov007Mdl_SetCb`) compiled to `ldr rX, =0xffdf; and`. The target has `bic r0, r0, #0x20`. Writing it as `FLD(u16, m, 0xc2) = FLD(u16, m, 0xc2) & ~0x20;` gave the `bic`, and it also fixed the order of the hoisted constants in the prologue (`mov r6, #2` before `mov r4, #0x64`): 30 to 20. A `(s32)` cast on the left operand does the same. `(u16)~0x20`, `~0x20u`, `~(u16)0x20`, `0xffdf`, `~(1 << 5)` and an `s16` field did not. The round big10 note found the `bic` only through a temporary (which recoloured everything) or a 1-bit field. When the target has `bic #imm` on a halfword field and the draft loads a mask from the pool, expand the compound assignment. `scratchpad/big11/t/cmpd.py src func module` expands each compound assignment of a draft in turn and scores it. On the other nine drafts it found nothing.
- **Use the setter inline that the other cases already use.** One case of `021ce038` wrote `s->sub = 100; s->unk_58 = 0xe10; s->unk_54 = 0; s->unk_50 = 1; s->idx = 4;` by hand. The target's store order is that of the existing `Ov007_Go(s, idx, kind)` (`unk_58`, `unk_54`, `unk_50`, `idx`, `sub`), and calling `Ov007_Go(s, 4, 1)` there swapped two callee-saved registers into place (34 to 30). The sequence diff had shown only a register swap, not the store order.
- **Re-check old choices after later changes.** The round big10 draft used `w != i` as the ring-scan loop test, because it had been better (58 to 44) before the type scan added `long w`. With `long w`, `i != w` is better: 42 to 34, and the scans' `cmp` operand order is now the target's.
- **The first scan of `021ce038` can reuse the caller's pointer.** A second copy of the scan inline that takes the queue pointer (`Ov007_HasMsgP(q0, r, w, 2)`, with the other three scans still reading the global through `volatile`) gives the target's code there (no reload). It costs one register swap of `q` and `i` in that loop (20 to 19). The parameter order of the copy, `while`/`for` forms and `long` on `r`/`w` did not change that swap.
- **Still open in `021ce038`: `Ov007Cam_SetA`.** The four temporaries (the constant table pointer, `x`, `0xaaa`, `0xeff1`) are coloured in exactly the reverse of the target's order (`r3, r2, r1, r0` against `r0, r1, r2, r3`). All 360 statement orders of the inline body, reversed or extra parameters for the two constants, a pointer local in the caller, an `s16 *` parameter and an `extern` symbol in place of the constant address did not change it.
- **`u16 k` in `func_ov011_021ff24c`'s ring lookup.** The target truncates the clamped index (`subhs; lslhs; lsrhs`) and then computes the slot with `sublo; addlo; subhs`. `u16 k = i;` in `F24c_RingAt` and a `u16 k` parameter in `F24c_Idx` give exactly those instructions. The type scan had made both `s32` (76) because the count is lower, and `u16` scores 78. It is the target's code, so later work should start from `scratchpad/big11/d/f24c2.cpp`. On top of it, `BOOL r = TRUE; if (e != 2 && e != 3) r = FALSE;` gives the target's `mov r3, r4; cmp #2 ...; movne r3, r5` shape for `F24c_IsMove`, with `cmpne` where the target has `beq`. The rest of that draft gets worse (100).
- **Pair-swap climbs (`hc.py`) changed nothing here.** On the declaration blocks of `021b2f08` (20 lines, 200 rounds), `02166be8` (11) and `021fb1b4` (11), no order scored differently from the draft. These functions' register assignments do not depend on declaration order.
- **Reversed inline parameters changed nothing here either.** `scratchpad/big11/t/ip.py src func module [inline ...]` tries every parameter order of each inline (up to 120 per inline) and rewrites the call sites to match. It ran on all ten drafts and found nothing. In `func_ov011_02204428` the `FX_Atan2` argument loads stay at 6 diffs whatever the order in the source: a wrapper with reversed parameters, locals in either order, a 4-argument inline in all orders, pointer locals and `VEC_Subtract`-style inlines. MWCC's scheduler reorders those loads. Reading both vectors through `volatile` gives the target's load order but not its registers (5 to 7 diffs, depending on the order of the locals). The same holds for the call-argument `mov` order in `func_ov000_021ad8ac`: all 120 parameter orders of a wrapper around `func_01ffc128`, and every position of the `tgt` store, keep 6 or more. About 600 permuter iterations on `02204428` (with the ov011 header copied in and `vNN` renamed `vfunc_NN`) found nothing.
- **`func_ov010_021fe2a4`: `for (i = 0; ...)` inside case `0x14` gives the target's entry block** (`mov r6, #0; mov sb, r6` and `mov sl, sb` in the case). But then MWCC drops the second `cmp r2, #0x14` of the switch tree, which the target keeps (25, `scratchpad/big11/d/e2a4_af0.cpp`). A switch on a local, an `s32` cast, `flag` assigned as a statement and a block-scoped `i` did not bring the `cmp` back.
- **`func_ov011_021fc0b8` (13): the `1` passed as `on` lives in `sl` in the target, and the zero for the list removal is set again in the inner loop's preheader.** In the draft the zero is hoisted to `sl` and the `1` is set just before the calls. Reversed `Ov011Ent_Remove` parameters, calling it with `self` or `self->ent`, the two zero stores in either order or chained, a `while` loop, an early return, and `mover.py` on nine statements of case 2 did not change it.
## Copying sibling code into undrafted functions (ov000 and ov003, round ovhi18)

- **Start an undrafted function from a matched sibling.** Five functions matched in this round, four of them at the first or second compile, by copying a matched neighbour's structs and inlines and editing only what the target does differently. Near-misses with many earlier rounds of variants moved much less. Before you start a fresh function, look for one that has the same call sequence (the same `vfunc_XX` offsets, the same `NNS_G3dGeBufferOP_N` command list).
  - `func_ov000_021593b8` and `func_ov000_02158588` (list draw) are copies of `func_ov000_021561f8` (`Lo_02155f68.cpp`) with fewer branches. In `02158588`, two edits fixed the last diffs. First, `if (top->next2 == NULL) k = 0; else k = -1;` written in each branch gives the target's `moveq #0; mvnne #0`. The `? :` form, `k--`, and `!= NULL` with the arms swapped each leave 4 to 8 diffs. Second, the selected item `self->a8` needs its own pointer local (`sel`). Reusing the `top` local swaps two registers.
  - `func_ov000_02155770` (list input) inlines a copy of the `func_ov000_02152294` body. The inlined factory only matched with `void *res = data_02149ef8;` declared before `VObj *p;`.
  - `func_ov000_02153804` (G3 window frame, 17 aligned diffs, `scratchpad/ovhi18/d/3804/s2.cpp`) uses the `G3_Vec`/`G3_Cmd1`/`G3_Vtx`/`G3_VtxXY` inlines of `func_ov000_02155f68`. The `v[n - 1]` index the target keeps in a register is `n = self->unk70 - 1;` computed once. Inline arguments are evaluated right to left, so `G3_VtxXY(V[2].x, -t)` (with `V` the field read, not a local copy) computes `-t` before it loads the array, as in the target. What remains is one swap of `r8` and `sb` between the `FX_Div` result `t` and the divisor `sy`. Declaration-order and type climbs (`hc2.py`) did not move it.
- **Bit tests that look like bit-fields.** `func_ov000_02155770` tests `tst #0x10000` on the flags word. The bit-field `LBIT` form used elsewhere in the file did not match here. Write the mask test, `LFLD(u32, self, 0xc) & 0x10000`.
- **Count the arguments of a virtual call from the registers.** When a `blx` through a vtable slot has `r3` (or a stack slot) set just before it, the method takes more arguments than its siblings. `vfunc_b0`/`vfunc_b8` in `02155770` take `(LFLD(s32, self, 0x20), 0)`. The `0` that looks like dead code is the second argument.
- **Where a constant is defined decides which register it is built from.** In `func_ov003_021ed15c` (homing projectile), case 1 sets the speed and the vertical velocity. MWCC builds `0x29` from the register that holds `0x2e1` only if `speed = 0x2e1;` comes before `self->vel.y = 0x29;` in the source. In the other order it builds the constants the other way round.
- **A fourth function with pooled G3 constants.** `func_ov000_0215b598` (sprite G3 draw) loads `0x11`, `0`, `0x10000`, `0x1c`, `3`, `0x1b`, `0x19`, `0xc` and `0x800` from the literal pool. These are exactly the arguments of the push, translate, scale, `MTX_Scale43_`, `MTX_Concat43` and mult-matrix calls. The commands around them (`0x29`, `0x40`, `0x20`, `0x23`, `0x25`) are still `mov`. This is the same blocker as `021535a8`/`0215d4b4`/`0215b2e4` (see above). Two more forms were tried and failed: float-to-int constants (`(int)17.0f`) and `static const` arrays. The draft is otherwise structurally complete (`scratchpad/ovhi18/d/b598/s0.cpp`).
- **Small results from this round's near-misses.** In `func_ov003_021e44dc`, passing the `Ov003Big_Load` parameters as `(ext, name)` takes it from 7 to 2 (`scratchpad/ovhi18/d/44dc/v_rev.cpp`). It still needs the shared ov003 string pool. In `func_ov002_021e6d4c`, removing the `pp` pointer local takes it from 43 to 13 (`scratchpad/ovhi18/d/6d4c/a.cpp`). In `func_ov015_021fce94`, writing the stores as "SetFovyND, n, f, dirty" gets the store order right, but it then rotates `r1`/`r2`/`r3` (24 against the base 13; `scratchpad/ovhi18/d/ce94/p0132.cpp`). `func_ov006_021dacb0` (SlotManager create, new draft, 41) is right in structure. The target keeps the slot offset `i * 0x4c` and recomputes `p + off` after `MI_CpuFill8`, while ours keeps `p + off` live across the call. Reset, ctor and fill inlines did not change that (`scratchpad/ovhi18/d/acb0/s0.cpp`).

## TU2 blockers, round 4 (round mainpool7)

No TU2 blocker matched this round, so TU2 is still not merged. Drafts are in `scratchpad/mainpool7/d/`.

- **Coloring order of virtual registers.** Small test functions (`scratchpad/mainpool7/exp/`) show the order MWCC assigns registers when nothing is under pressure. Compiler temporaries (constants, CSE and hoisted values) come first, then locals in declaration order, then parameters, last parameter first. Each one takes the lowest free register. A copy `T *p = param;` is coalesced into the parameter and keeps the parameter's place. An inline's parameters and locals rank as locals of the caller. So a parameter only gets a lower register than a local when the graph is under pressure (spills). That is the case in `func_0205a358`, where the target has `pool` in `r4` and the entry in `r5`. Nothing found this round reverses the order there.
- **`func_0205e5c4` (19 to 2, `d/e5c4_best2.cpp`).** Three changes:
  - Declaring `u32 lo;` before `int type;` (assigned separately) moved `id & 0xf000` into `lr` and removed the extra `sb` push.
  - The parameter block is set up by an inline in the style of `SpawnInit021b5dc0` (`src/ov000/Mgr_021b5dc0.cpp`): `LightParam_Init(&param, 0xffff)` writes `key = id`, `mask = id & 0xf000`, clears `unk_04[4]` and sets `unk_0c = -1`. The draft's hand-written `0xffff`/`0xf000` stores and the `-1` written after the key both go away.
  - The two final writes use different shift forms: the key is `lo | ((u32)(type << 28) >> 16)` and the mask is `((u32)type << 28) >> 16`. Because the two expressions differ, they are not CSE'd, which gives the target's `lsr r3`/`mvn ip` registers and its store order.

  `hcm.py` (below) then climbed the function, block and statement orders to 2. The last 2 are the order of `data_02149efc.unk_14 = NULL` and `unk_04 = NULL`. Only the source order `unk_04` then `unk_14` gives the target's registers, but the target stores `unk_14` first. Writing `unk_14` first, a chained assignment, an inline setter or a pointer local each lengthen the life of the `&data_02149efc` register (the second store is scheduled later), and that costs 13–15. With `res = slot->res` and both stores before the `{ id ...}` block (`d/e5/g1.cpp`, 9), the registers and the store order are right, but `id` is then loaded after the stores.
- **`func_02060b18` (21 to 17, `d/b18_best17.c`).** Two changes:
  - The `0a08` trick: a function-scope `StageFlags *f;`, assigned in each branch.
  - Declaring `u32 i;` before `u32 key = ...` in the clear inline (the reverse of what was tried in round 3 without `f`).

  The second branch is now correct except for one `and` pair and an `orr` operand order. The first branch still has `f` in `r3` (target `lr`).
- **`func_0205c234` (2): the load is placed by the scheduler, not by the statement position.** The draft is one instruction early on `ldr r0, [r0, #0x18]`. Wherever `start = data_02149efc.slotStart` goes, the address `ldr` is scheduled at most one store before the value load. In the target there are two stores between them. A pointer local or a `volatile s32 *` to the field, at any pair of positions, a `u16 *args` pointer for the stores, and `args[3]` before the load all keep 2.
- **`func_0205b1b4` (2).** `MI_CpuFill8`'s `0` is coalesced into `r1`. The function pointer moves to `r2` (the target) only when the zero is materialised before the handler store. In the source, that happens only when a zero store comes first, and then the stores come out in the wrong order. Two inlines with reversed parameters (`Init(0, func)`, `Init(&g, func, 0)`) and a `Reset(g, slots)` inline still gave 2. `-O4,s`, `-O3` and the other compiler versions don't help.
- **`func_02059c98`: the `stm r4!` run is `MTX_Identity33_`.** The target's `mov r1, #0x1000; mov r2, #0; mov r3, #0; stm r4!, {r1, r2}; stm r4!, {r2, r3}; ...; stm r4!, {r1}` writes a 3×3 identity into the matrix at `sp+4`, after the translation at `+0x24`. It looks like an inlined asm identity (the real `MTX_Identity33_` is at `0x02011340` and uses the same `stm` pairs). An `asm { }` block with the matrix pointer bound to a register is the next thing to try.
- **Unchanged this round:** `0205e29c` (12), `02060a08` (17) and `0205a358` (71). The `e29c` attempts were reversed `GetArea` parameters, the index as `int`/`u32`, `unk_80 = 0` first, and `Stage_FindObj3D` as a local-variable, early-return or ternary inline (139: the inline shape changes the code). The `0a08` attempts were 192 key and parameter forms combined with the `f` local, and a `u16 v` copy of `args[1]` (29). For `a358`, 40 minutes of the permuter (11 700 iterations) found nothing. So did `hcm.py` on the 10 inline declarations plus `e`/`next`/`pl`, inlines taking `(e, pool)`, the loop in an inline (it then exceeds the inline depth and `CheckBox` is no longer inlined), and `x`/`y`/`z`/`unk_18` as loop-body locals.
- **Tools.** `scratchpad/mainpool7/t/hcm.py src func module iters block1 [block2 ...]` is `hc.py` over several blocks at once. Each step swaps or moves lines inside one random block. `HCCONS="1:2<3"` keeps line 2 of block 1 before line 3. `t/matchx.py` is `match.py` with extra compiler flags from `MWX`. A climb's `.hcN` output file needs the `.cpp` extension back before `match.py` compiles it as C++.
- **Backgrounding.** `cd dir && nohup cmd &` backgrounds the whole `&&` list, so `$!` is a subshell and not the permuter. Run `cd` as its own command, then `nohup cmd &` on its own line, so `$!` is the process you need to stop later.

## TU1 blockers, round 4: the VRAM bases are variables (main, round mainpool8)

- **`func_02051210` and `func_0205592c` matched.** The VRAM-free loop's bases are variables that the optimizer did not propagate, and the loop index is a `long`. The working form defines the bases twice, the second time under a condition that is never true at run time:

  ```c
  long i;
  u8 *objBase;
  u8 *subObjBase;
  objBase = (u8 *)0x6400000;
  subObjBase = (u8 *)0x6600000;
  if ((u32)win & 3) {
      objBase = (u8 *)0x6400000;
      subObjBase = (u8 *)0x6600000;
  }
  ...
  MIi_CpuClearFast(0, (b->kind == 4 ? objBase : subObjBase) + b->addr, b->size - b->pad);
  ```

  The optimizer does not propagate a variable that has two definitions into the arms of a `?:` (it does propagate it into arithmetic, see below). The backend then sees that the second assignment is redundant and deletes it together with the `if` and its condition, so nothing of it is left in the code. A variable gets a register above the locals, which is the target's order (zero, `i`, `0x6400000`, `0x6600000`, then the parameter). This only works with `long i`: with `int i` the index still gets the highest register (`i` r7). A `long` index also does this in small tests where the bases are hoisted non-constant expressions (`q * 3`, `q * 5`), so the index type affects the order whenever the bases are variables. In `02051210` the definitions go at the top of the function, which also puts both `mov`s in the entry block as in the target. In `0205592c` (not a TU1 function; it fills the gap after `FieldMsg.cpp`, whose entry now ends at `0x020559c8`) they go after the bitfield clears.
- **The condition has to leave no trace.** `if (cache)` leaves a `movs`. A condition that loads (`cache->blocks[0]`) or reads the value that is being stored (`cache->count`) is scheduled before the `if` is removed and moves the bitfield code to `r1`. A condition on the bases themselves (`a != c`) pulls the `mov`s above the store. `(u32)cache & 3` and `i == 0x7f` (on the not-yet-initialised index) leave nothing.
- **`func_0204f524` (3) and `func_0204f5bc` (1) do not match yet.** Here the target also stores `numBlocks` from the index register before the bases: `mov r5, #0; strb r5, [win, #0x3bc]; mov r6, #A; mov r7, #B; mov r4, r5`. Every order of the four statements (`i = 0`, the store, the definitions, the `if`) misses one detail. With the `if` after `i = 0` (`ISDU`), the hoisted loop zero is `mov r4, #0` instead of a copy of `i`, because the backend only reuses a register that holds 0 when the definition is in the loop preheader, and the removed `if` leaves the preheader as a separate block until after that pass. In `f524` this also swaps the first two instructions. With the definitions and the `if` before `i = 0` (`DUIS`), the copy is there but the scheduler puts the two `mov`s before the `strb`. Storing `0` instead of `i` before the definitions (`SDUI`, the form that matches `0205592c`) gives the store its own `mov r0, #0`. Things that did not help: a second `i = 0` after the `if`, a duplicate definition of `i`, `if`/`else` with the same definitions on both sides (propagated), redefinitions inside the loop (the backend keeps them), `?:` and self-assignments (folded), address-taken tricks, empty `asm {}`, defining the bases in the loop, and passing them as parameters to an inline.
- **No other way to make a constant into a variable worked.** These were all propagated or folded and gave constant ordering: `static const` (C and C++), `const` locals, single-member and two-member struct locals, unions (these use a stack frame), `u64`, `float` and `s64` casts, `&*(u8 *)K`, `((VramMap *)0x6000000)->obj`, a local plus an offset, inlines that return the base, and conditions that fold only after propagation (`int z = 0; if (z) ...`, because the optimizer runs to a fixpoint). Non-const file statics and globals are loaded. Arithmetic on a two-definition variable is not folded by the backend (`lsl r6, r1, #1` for `v * 2`).
- **`func_0204ffdc` (52): the same trick does not reach `dy`.** `if ((u32)box & 3) dy = 0;` changes nothing: the optimizer still propagates `dy` into `y = dy + ...` (arithmetic uses are propagated even with two definitions), and the backend writes `add r2, r0, #0`. A `long dy` keeps it as a variable but puts the duplicate's block between `dy` and `busy`, so `busy` gets its own `mov r5, #0` and the diff grows to 155.
- **`func_02051a34` (35) and `func_020508f4` (8):** the type scan (`ts.py`, whose type list includes `long`) found nothing for either. In `1a34` the hoisted constant is the mask `0xffff0000` that the compiler makes for the 16-bit `textPos` bitfield store, also used as `-1` via `asr #16`. It does not appear in the source, so the two-definition trick does not apply to it.
## An `s64` accumulator, block-local CSE of a global, and unsigned ring indexes (all overlays, round big12)

`func_ov000_02166be8` matched this round (new file `src/ov000/Lo_02166be8.cpp`). Drafts are in `scratchpad/big12/d/` and `scratchpad/big12/best/`.

- **Build a sum of squares in an `s64` accumulator: `func_ov000_02166be8` matched (29 to 0).** The distance check `sq = (s32)(((s64)dx * dx + (s64)dz * dz + 0x800) >> 12);` gave the target's `smull`/`smlal` order but swapped the registers of `dx`, `dz` and the product. Writing `(s64)dz * dz + (s64)dx * dx` gave the target's registers but the wrong multiply order (19). `{ s64 t = (s64)dx * dx; t += (s64)dz * dz; sq = (s32)((t + 0x800) >> 12); }` matches. A single expression `s64 t = a + b;` does not (29), and neither does accumulating in the other order (36). When a `smull`/`smlal` chain has the right instructions but the wrong registers, try splitting it into an accumulator and `+=` statements.
- **MWCC only CSEs a global inside one basic block. `func_ov007_021ce038` went from 19 to 10.** The four message-queue scans read `data_ov000_021ccd54` again in each scan. The round big11 draft used a `volatile` read in each scan and passed the caller's pointer to the first scan only. That cost one register swap in the first scan. In the target, the first scan's queue pointer and the caller's `q0` are one register, because both loads are in the same basic block. So the first scan is the same inline with a plain (non-`volatile`) read, `Ov007_HasMsgN(r, w, 2)`. The other three scans keep the `volatile` read. Without `volatile` there, MWCC hoists the global into a callee-saved register and every register in the function changes (486). When a reloaded global and a caller's copy of it share a register in the target, read it with a plain load in the first block and with `volatile` in later blocks.
- **Still open in `021ce038`: `Ov007Cam_SetA` (10, `best/ce038_10.cpp`).** These forms kept the reversed order of the four temporaries:
  - every declaration order of `x`, `u16 a = 0xaaa` and `s32 b = 0xeff1` as locals, with `k` as a parameter or as a local;
  - the constants passed as inline parameters in four orders, with `x` declared, split from its declaration or left out;
  - the address as a literal in the body (MWCC then folds `+0x54` into the pool constant);
  - all 24 store orders with literal addresses;
  - `x`/`y`/`k` as caller locals or inline parameters.

  On a 30-line reproduction (`scratchpad/big12/x/a.cpp`, compiled by `t/mc.sh`), 432 combinations of these forms (`x/gen.py`) never gave the target's `k r0, x r1, 0xeff1 r2, 0xaaa r3`. Inlining the body by hand also kept 10. The local permuter scripts cannot convert the ov007 header (`v04` virtuals), so the permuter was not run.
- **`func_ov000_02171478` (11): `u32 index = (u32)i < MESSAGE_QUEUE_LEN ? i : i - MESSAGE_QUEUE_LEN;`** gives the target's `mov r1, r2; cmp r2, #0x10; subhs r1, r2, #0x10` (`best/1478_11t.cpp`). Only the `end`/`index` registers (`r1`/`sl` swapped) and one scheduled `lsr` are still wrong. The `if` form `index = i; if (index >= LEN) index -= LEN;` gives the same instructions, but it moves every register in the scan (23). The type scan and `hc2.py` on that `if` version reached 22. These all kept the swap:
  - all orders of the `end`/`read`/`i`/`index` declarations, with `end` as `long`, `s32` or `u32`;
  - moving the scan into an inline that takes `end`/`read` as parameters, in all 24 orders;
  - a `Message *m` local, `index` inlined into the subscript, and no `read` local.
- **Unchanged this round:**
  - `func_ov011_02204428` (6): the address of `data_02092a38` always gets `r0` (target `r2`). This held for ten more source forms, including accumulated `dx -=`, negated adds, pointer locals and copies of the four fields, and for the same forms in a reproduction (`x/at.cpp`).
  - `func_ov000_021ad8ac` (6): the stack argument `str sl, [sp]` is scheduled before `mov r0, #imm` in two branches. `EaseTo` inlines that take the destination, a local for the first argument, `-0x1200` and `(s16)` casts did not move it.
  - `func_ov011_021fc0b8` (13): a function-scope `on`, `u8`/`s16`/`u16` `on`, `TRUE` passed directly, an early-return or `return` form of `Ov011Ent_Remove`, and `on = TRUE` inside the `if` all gave 13.
  - `func_ov010_021fe2a4` (22): the target keeps the second `cmp r2, #0x14` of the switch tree. MWCC removes the same second compare in the `0x1f` subtree in both builds. A reproduction keeps neither. In the real function, changing the case-`0x14` loop to run zero times brings the compare back, so the loop shape decides it. `while`, `do`, `continue`, `i = 1..3` and block-scoped `i` did not keep it while also keeping `mov sl, sb` in the case. The target also loads `&data_ov010_0220ecb0` twice in that case (ours keeps it in `r5`). Neither an address inline, a distance or move inline, nor a `volatile` cast changed that.
- **Tools.** `scratchpad/big12/t/mc.sh file.cpp` compiles a reproduction with the overlay flags and disassembles it with `llvm-objdump`. `x/run.py`, `x/gen.py` and `x/atrun.py` run variant lists through it. `t/cmpchk.py` counts a given compare at a function's start.
## TU2 blockers, round 5 (round mainpool9)

`func_02059c98` and `func_0205a358` matched, so 6 TU2 blockers are left and TU2 is still not merged. Drafts are in `scratchpad/mainpool9/d/`. The best ones are `e5c4_best2.cpp` (2), `c234v3.c` (2), `b1b4_min.c` (2), `e29c_g.cpp` (9), `a08x_if.c` (13) and `b18w_16.c` (16).

- **An `asm` block in a function turns off part of the optimiser for the whole function.** `func_02059c98` (box collision mesh, now at the end of `ObjPool.cpp`, whose `delinks.txt` entry grows to `0x0205a028`) writes its translation-plus-identity matrix with an inline asm block in `Box_SetMtx`:

  ```c
  asm {
      str x, [m, #0x24]
      mov r1, #0x1000
      str y, [m, #0x28]
      ...
      stmia m!, {r1}
  }
  ```

  The translation stores have to be in the asm block too. When they are C statements before it, `y` and `z` get `r1`/`r2` instead of `r2`/`r3`. Adding the block (122 to 60 aligned diffs) also fixed two problems from round 2 that had nothing to do with the matrix. The bit-field writes kept their `bic #8; orr #8` and `bic #2; orr #0` pairs, and both face loops stopped being rotated (`b` to the `cmp`). So when a target has unrotated constant loops or `bic`/`orr` pairs that fold in every C form, look for an asm block somewhere in the same function. The rest of `9c98` was:
  - `s = e->unk_18; if (s < e->unk_1c) s = e->unk_1c; if (s < e->unk_20) s = e->unk_20;` for the `movlt` max (the ternary form gives `movge`);
  - both face loops written out in the function with a function-level `int i;` declared right after `e`, and `tri += 2` in the `for` increment. This swapped `res` and the `tail`/`i` register (`sb`/`r8`). A loop in an inline with its own `i`, and every declaration order in an `hcm.py` climb, kept the swap.
  - The three tables are now `data_02082a10`, `data_02082a78` and `data_02082b08` in `symbols.txt` (`kind:data(any)`, like `data_02082a28`). They are still in the delinked `.rodata` gap, so the TU2 merge still has to take over `0x02082a10`..`0x02082b98`.
- **Explicit locals for loop invariants: `func_0205a358` matched** (now at the end of `ObjPool_2.cpp`, which grows to `0x0205a69c`). Round 4 found no way to swap `pool` (`r4`) and the entry (`r5`). What fixed it was in the `ObjPool_CheckBox` inline: copy `entry->x`, `y`, `z` and `unk_18` into locals before the loop and pass the copy of `unk_18` to a box test (`ObjPool_InBoxW(e, dx, dy, dz, w)`) that still reads `unk_1c`/`unk_20` from the entry. The loop is `i = 0; if (i < n) { x = ...; w = ...; do { ... } while (++i < n); }`, so the copies are loaded after the `n > 0` test, where the target loads them. With a plain `for`, the copies are loaded before the test. A script tried all 120 orders of `s32 w; z; y; x; i;`, and only `w, z, y, x, i` gives 0 (4 to 9 otherwise). The target's 4 invariants are allocated after the loop index, the way named locals are. Values that LICM hoists are allocated before the constants. If a loop's hoisted loads have the "wrong" registers, try them as named locals and search the declaration order.
- **`func_02060b18` (17 to 16, `d/b18w_16.c`).** With the clear loop written out in the function and `u16 a; u32 key; u32 i; StageFlags *f;` at function scope, the first branch's `f` and `i` are right. What is left is that the target keeps the condition's `args[1]` in `ip` (ours `r2`), plus the operand order of the key `orr`, in both branches. These made no difference: a `v = args[1]` local (every type and declaration position), all 30 parameter orders and key expressions of `Flags_MakeKey2`, and a permuter run from this draft (its only find read an uninitialised pointer). The same function-level loop form for `func_02060a08` gives 82 or worse, because the early return in `Flags_FindFreeSlot` is part of the shape.
- **`func_02060a08` (17 to 13, `d/a08x_if.c`).** Declare the flags pointer inside `Flags_FindFreeSlot`, after its loop index (`u32 i; StageFlags *flags = data_0214a424;`), and take no parameter. `f` then gets `r4`, as in the target. Declaring it before `i` gives 19. What is left is the order of the key bytes: the target has `d` in `ip` and `a` in `r3` in the first branch, and `a` in `lr` and `b` in `r4` in the second. All 24 orders of the `Flags_AddSlot` parameters, with 3 key expressions each, a `key` local and `u32` copies of the bytes stayed at 13 or worse.
- **`func_0205e5c4` (2).** Every source order in which `unk_14` is stored before `unk_04` scores 4 or more. All 120 orders of `id`, `type`, `res`, the two stores and `lo` were tried. The best (4, `d/e5_r4.cpp`) has the target's registers, but it loads `id` after the stores (`res = slot->res;` and the two stores first). Also tried with no effect: chained and `volatile` forms, a globals pointer, putting the `unk_04` store inside `LightParam_Init`, and declaring `id` at function scope.
- **`func_0205e29c` (9 sequence diffs).** The `NULL` for `unk_14` reuses the zero of `unk_80 = 0`. That merged value is in `r3` in the target, with `args[2]` in `r2`, and the other way round in ours. These made no difference: an explicit `void *obj` (function scope at every position, in the inline, or as a `NULL` initialiser plus `if`), and `args[2]`, `res` or the zero as named locals. Writing the 3D lookup without the second `id != 0` test changes the shape (22).
- **`func_0205b1b4` (2) and `func_0205c234` (2).** In `b1b4`, a zero store before the handler store gives the target's registers. But the scheduler never moves a store above an earlier one, even when the two are different globals and both are defined in the file. Inserting a label, `goto`, `do { } while (0)`, `switch` or `if (1)` block boundaries after the handler store did not move the zero's definition up. An empty `asm { }` block changed nothing. In `c234`, loads stay after the stores before them in the source, and the reloaded `&data_02149efc` is scheduled one store ahead of its use. The target has it two stores ahead.
## Constants in branch locals, and `s32` for a bit-field read (overlays 2 and 15, round ovhi19)

- **A constant assigned in both arms of an `if` becomes a variable.** `func_ov002_021e6d4c` (13 diffs, `scratchpad/ovhi18/d/6d4c/a.cpp`) picks `y` and `pal` in an `if`/`else` and then passes `10` as the x of a position inline. In the target, `10` goes to `r0`, the reloads of `data_02097ce0` go to `r1`/`r2`, and the zero for the `+8` store (`r4`) is set before the first store. Declaring `s16 x;` and writing `x = 10;` in both arms (after `y` and `pal`) matches; any type for `x` works. `x = 10;` before the `if`, between the stores, or as an initialiser all stay at 13. This is the same effect as the round ovhi18 `k = 0` / `k = -1` in each branch: a value set in the branches is a variable at the join and is coloured as one, not as a constant that is rebuilt where it is used.
- **`s32` for a bit-field value keeps the shift in the operand.** `func_ov015_021fb25c` (digit glyphs, 16 diffs) reads the 10-bit `w` field of the last glyph and later computes `w - (val % 10) * 2`. With `s16 w` the extraction is done up front (`lsl; lsr #22`). The target keeps `w << 22` in `ip` and folds the `lsr #22` into the `rsb`. `s32 w` gives that code (29 diffs, the other registers are rotated). `combo2.py` then found the rest at the first step: `s32 n;` declared after `u16 i;` instead of before it (0). The round ovhi18 lead (moving the `val` load below `w = last->w`) is safe: both are loads with no store between them. When a target uses a field's shifted value as an operand (`, lsr #N`), try a 32-bit type for the local before reordering anything.
- **Still open this round:** `func_ov000_02153804` (17): the `r8`/`sb` swap did not change with `t` declared before `sy`, with `sy` or `sx` computed in place (`sy <<= 12`), or with `FX_Div` in an inline (17 each). A `combo2.py` round (1019 distinct neighbours) found nothing below 17. `func_ov006_021dacb0` (41): a C++ class with `operator new` and an inline constructor, indexed `p->slots[j].f` stores, and a separate `SubClr` inline for `sub2C`/`sub3C` all keep `p + off` across `MI_CpuFill8`. `func_ov015_021fce94` (13): `Ov015SetFovy` (with `dirty` inside) in place of `Ov015SetFovyND` plus the stores gives 19 to 62, and `hcm.py` over the stores of `Ov015Cam_Init`, the inner `Persp` block and the four `0x18c` stores (300 steps) found nothing. `func_ov013_022001b8` (3): `this`-like `&ref != NULL &&` first terms are not folded (12), and `bool` inlines with early returns, ternaries and locals of every type stay at 3. `func_ov003_021e1204` (2): a pointer inline `EKind(e)` and `u16`/`s32` copies of `unk_16` stay at 2. `func_ov002_021e6508` (2): `FX_MUL` as an inline with either parameter order, and the multiply in each `switch` arm (19), do not change the load order. `func_ov003_021de6b4` (6): the `0x325` argument as a local, the scale vector built in an argument inline or a comma expression, and other parameter types stay at 6. `func_ov010_0220640c` (6): the zero argument as a local assigned at each statement of case 0, and the stores moved into an inline with the call's arguments as parameters, stay at 6. `func_ov000_021be5dc` (3), `021e0ff8` (2), `021e2690` (2), `02211b2c` (2), `021ead50` (2), `021d883c` (3) and `func_ov006_021e1bb4` (5) did not move either: the `z - 0x1000` local in a new inline, `i = 0` before `by` and `do`/`while` loops, `Ov003Vec` (with its constructor) for the copied vector, and expression or compound forms of both `w`/`h` subtractions in the caption draw.

## A pool constant that was a table entry, and ov007 as one string-pool file (all overlays, round big13)

`func_ov007_021ce038` matched this round, which completes overlay 7. Drafts are in `scratchpad/big13/best/`.

- **`0x02071160` was `FX_SinCosTable_ + 0x100`, not a literal address. This fixed `Ov007Cam_SetA` (10 to 0).** The draft wrote `const u8 *k = (const u8 *)0x02071160` and read `k + 0x54`/`k + 0x56`, and every form of that kept the four temporaries in reverse register order. The pool word is `FX_SinCosTable_` (`0x02071060`) plus `0x100`, because `ldrsh` only takes an 8-bit offset, so the real offset `0x154` is split into `0x100 + 0x54`. `0x154 / 4 = 0x55` is the sin/cos pair for angle `0x555`, half of the `0xaaa` that is stored next to it (a camera field of view and its half-angle sine and cosine). Writing `FX_SinCosTable_[(0x555 >> 4) * 2]` and `[(0x555 >> 4) * 2 + 1]` gives the target's registers and load order directly. When a pool word is near a known table, subtract the table's address and check whether the remainder is an index the code already uses. A literal address and a symbol plus offset get different registers.
- **Overlay 7's strings belong to one file, so `ov007_021ccda0.cpp` now covers `.text 0x021ccda0..0x021cf474` and `.data 0x021cfbd8..0x021cfc60`.** `021ce038` needs real string literals (the target keeps `"pic/titlemdl"` in a saved register, see round mainbig6; `extern` names gave 50). Its strings sit in one packed pool that starts with `"pic/license_en"` (used by `021ccf9c`) and also holds `"pic/title"`, which `021cef80` uses as well. So the four old files `021ccda0`, `021cd628`, `021cdff0` and `021cef80` are merged with `021ce038` into one file with `#pragma pool_strings on`. Every function in it still matches. The other changes:
  - `symbols.txt`: the `data_ov007_021cfbd8`..`021cfc5c` names become `@stringBase0 kind:data(byte[0x87]) addr:0x021cfbd8 local`.
  - `relocs.txt`: the 14 relocations into the pool are rewritten as `to:0x021cfbd8 add:0x..`. `dsd` prints the right form in its error.
  - `include/ov007/ov007.h`: the `data_ov007_021cfbd8` extern is removed.
  
  The literal pool is 0x87 bytes and the range ends at `0x021cfc60`. That range passes the ROM check. The `celog...` strings and the table at `0x021cfbb4` (before `0x021cfbd8`) are still unclaimed.
- **`func_ov011_021ff24c`: wrong inline shapes (22 to 10 structural diffs, `best/f24c_s10.cpp`).** Comparing with registers ignored (`t/sdr.py`) showed that the ring loop was wrong in four places:
  - the clamp index `k` is `u16`. The target has `lslhs`/`lsrhs` after `cap - 1`, and then an unsigned `sublo`/`addlo`/`subhs` index;
  - the "move" test is two `if`s that return `TRUE` (`if (e == 2) return TRUE; if (e == 3) return TRUE;`). `e == 2 || e == 3` in any form becomes a range check (`sub`, `cmp #1`, `movls`);
  - the capacity is `u16 c = 0; BOOL e = !r->f.b0; if (!e) c = r->cap;`. MWCC then hoists `e` itself, not just the bit test;
  - `RingSize` returns `u32` (`blo`, not `blt`).
  
  Declaring `data_ov011_02207258` as non-`const` interleaves the final three copies as in the target. The aligned count is higher (96), because the loop registers moved. The one structural difference left is that the target reads `b0` again for the last `SetState` after `Count` on the same pointer. Passing the earlier local `l` to `SetState` gives the reload (6 structural, `best/f24c_s6_loc2.cpp`), but it also moves registers everywhere (186).
- **The two-definition trick did not help on overlay functions.** The `if ((u32)x & 3) v = v;` re-definition from round mainpool8 made things worse in every case tried. On `021fc0b8`, for `on`, the condition was hoisted out of the loop (`and r8, sb, #3`) or the constants were moved to new registers (53–149). On `02204428`, for either address, the removed `if` still split the block, so `&data_02092a50` was loaded again instead of being CSE'd from `r4` (27–98). On `021fe2a4`, `i = 0; if (i == 0x7f) i = 0;` inside case `0x14` gives the target's three entry `mov`s, but the second `cmp r2, #0x14` is still missing (25, `best/e2a4_entry.cpp`). Declaring `data_ov010_0220ecb0` as an array, `const`, or `s32`/`u8`/`char[]` with casts did not make the target load it again before `VEC_Distance`.
- **Unchanged:**
  - `02171478` (11): every order of the `end`/`read`/`i`/`index` declarations with `long`/`s32`/`u32` `end`, as well as `while`, `Message *m` and swapped-compare loops, gave 11. In `Task_Start50`, assignment and declaration orders, local types, `& 0xffff` forms and parameter types all gave 11 or more. Passing the four halves as `u16` inline parameters gave 163.

## TU2 blockers, round 6 (round mainpool11)

`func_0205b1b4` matched (now at the end of `StageRes_2.cpp`, whose `delinks.txt` entry grows to `0x0205b1fc` and now meets `StageRes_3.cpp`). Five TU2 blockers are left, so TU2 is still not merged. Drafts and test files are in `scratchpad/mainpool11/` (`d/`, `b18/`, `x/`).

- **A two-definition zero sets the handler store's register: `func_0205b1b4` matched.** The round mainpool8 trick also works for a zero that has to be set up before an unrelated store:

  ```c
  u32 zero;
  zero = 0;
  if ((u32)data_02149f18 & 3) {
      zero = 0;
  }
  data_0208bde8 = func_0205bef8;
  data_02149efc.slotStart = zero;
  ...
  MI_CpuFill8(data_02149f18, zero, sizeof(data_02149f18));
  ```

  The zero is now a variable defined above the handler store, so the function pointer can't share its register and goes to `r2`, as in the target. The backend deletes the redundant second definition and the `if`, so neither leaves any code. The stores stay in source order, and the scheduler still puts the `mov r1, #0` after the handler store. Before this, a zero store had to come first in the source, which put the stores in the wrong order.
- **The same trick does not help the other blockers.** The `if` splits the block until late in the backend. The zero reuse pass and the coloring run before the block is merged back, so they see the extra block:
  - In `func_0205c234` a two-definition address (`g = &data_02149efc;` after `args[1]`, `if ((u32)slot & 3) g = &data_02149efc;` after `args[5]`, `start = g->slotStart;`) gives the target's schedule: the address `ldr` comes right after `strh r1, [r4, #0xa]`, two stores ahead of the load. But the `NULL` in the `else` no longer reuses the zero register, and `slot` and the zero swap (`r4`/`r5`), so the result is 41. All 210 position pairs of the two lines (`t/ins2.py`) score 26 or worse. A constant address is always generated at its use, and the scheduler puts it exactly one store ahead of the load. That held in every reproduction (`x/s*.c`, compiled with `x/mc.sh`), whatever the pointer's origin or form. These forms all gave the same schedule: named-field unions for `args`, `u16 *` and laundered pointers, comma expressions, an inline taking `&data_02149efc` as a parameter, and dead or `volatile` early reads. The address only moves earlier when it gets a register that no earlier instruction in the block uses (it then goes to the top of the block).
  - In `func_0205e29c` a two-definition zero for `unk_80` is merged into the zero that the `Field_HasEvent` loop hoisted into `r4` (163 or worse, at every position). In `func_0205e5c4` a two-definition zero or address for the `unk_14`/`unk_04` stores gives 13 or worse.
- **`func_02060b18` (16): the key's registers follow the condition's `args[1]`.** The target keeps the condition's `args[1]` in `ip`. In ours it is in `r2`, and the `a`/`b` swap in both key computations follows from that. These all left the code unchanged at 16:
  - the 864 combinations of `u8`/`u16`/`u32`/`int` locals for the three key bytes (declaration order × assignment order × 3 key expressions);
  - all 6 parameter orders of `Flags_MakeKey2`, with `u8` and `u32` parameters and 5 key expressions;
  - `hi`/`lo` locals in branch 1, in every type and order.
  
  The permuter's only improvement (15) reads an uninitialised `StageResSlot *` copy, so it is not valid code. It does show that `args[1]` moves to `ip` when a second pointer to the slot is live in branch 2.
- **Unchanged:** `func_0205e5c4` (2), `func_0205c234` (2), `func_0205e29c` (9), `func_02060a08` (13) and `func_02060b18` (16). The permuter found nothing valid: about 26 minutes (3000–3500 iterations) each on `c234`, `0a08` and `b18`, and 10 minutes (1900 iterations) on `e5c4`. For `e5c4` this was the first run: `base.c` had to be turned into C by replacing each run of `virtual` methods with one `void *__vt;` and adding `typedef struct X X;` for every struct; the same conversion is in `pe29/`. Wrapping `GetArea` around a `GetArea2(res, i)` inline makes `e29c` worse (12).
- **Tools.** `scratchpad/mainpool11/t/ins2.py src func lo hi "lineA" ["lineB"]` inserts one line, or two lines in order, at every position between source lines `lo` and `hi`, and scores each result. `REPL="old|||new@@@..."` applies replacements first. Use it to place the two lines of the two-definition trick. `t/psetup.sh` and `t/prun.sh` are the round mainpool7 permuter scripts, pointed at this clone.
## Two-definition constants and loop indexes, and loads in tail-merged switch arms (overlays 2 and 3, round ovhi20)

`func_ov002_021e6508`, `func_ov002_021e0ff8` and `func_ov003_021de6b4` matched this round. Drafts are in `scratchpad/ovhi20/d/`.

- **A constant argument can be a two-definition variable too: `func_ov003_021de6b4` matched (6 to 0).** The round mainpool8 trick (define a variable a second time under `if ((u32)ptr & 3)`) is not only for VRAM bases. In this bomb-fuse tick the `0x325` argument came from the pool into `r2`, and the target has it in `r4`, after the three scale-vector constants. `kind = 0x325; if ((u32)self & 3) { kind = 0x325; }`, placed just before the `sc.x/y/z` stores, gives the target's registers. The same definitions at the top of the function, or right before the call, give 4. `t/tdc.py src func module` automates this: for every numeric literal in the function it tries the variable form at every statement position from the top down to the use (`TDNEAR=n` limits the distance, `TDT` the types). It found nothing else this round, but it is cheap to run on any function where a constant has the wrong register.
- **A loop index with two definitions keeps the preheader order: `func_ov002_021e0ff8` matched (2 to 0).** The target sets `i = 0` before `by = y + 0x14` in the loop preheader. Every statement order, `long` types, a `do`/`while` loop and `by` declared in the loop all gave `by` first. `i = 0; if ((u32)self & 3) { i = 0; } by = y + 0x14; do { ... } while (++i < 4);` matches. The two-definition `i` alone (with `for (; i < 4; i++)`) keeps the order right but brings back the entry test (`cmp r6, #4; bge`), because the optimizer no longer knows that `i` starts at 0, so the loop has to be a `do`/`while`.
- **Put a load in every arm of a switch to move it ahead of the join's loads: `func_ov002_021e6508` matched (2 to 0).** The target switches on the mode at `+0x84` with identical arms, then loads `+0x7c` before `+0x88` for an `FX_MUL`. The round ovhi18 draft read `+0x88` in each arm (`Rate6508`) and `+0x7c` after the switch, so the tail-merged `+0x88` load came first. The arms read `+0x7c` (`Ov002Anm_Frame`: `case 1: return FIELD(self, 0x7c, s32); case 0: ...; default: ...`), and the multiply is `f = Ov002Anm_Frame(self); f = FX_MUL(f, FIELD(self, 0x88, s32));`. The later `cmp lr, r0` against `+0x7c` still uses the same register, because the CSE sees the value from every arm. An empty switch is removed completely, and a switch whose arms all set the same index or pointer leaves `b` chains instead of jumping straight to the join. If tail-merged arms load the wrong field first, swap which value comes from the arms.
- **Smaller results from this round's near-misses (not matched):**
  - `func_ov006_021e1bb4` (5 to 3, `d/1bb4/s1.cpp`): a script tried all 720 orders of the six caption-draw declarations. `x, y, w, h, bx, by` puts `by` in `r1`. Only the `e->unk4` and `anim->unk48` registers are still swapped (`ip`/`sl`). `hcm.py` over the declarations and the eight statements, `combo2.py`, the types of `w`/`h`, and two-definition forms for `h` and the `unk48` value did not move them.
  - `func_ov003_021d883c` (3 to 2, `d/883c/v_p0fxd.cpp`): `Ov003Defaults *d = (Ov003Defaults *)&data_ov003_021f4980; s32 y; s32 x; x = d->v.x; y = d->v.y; if ((u32)d & 3) { x = d->v.x; } v.x = x; v.y = y; v.z = d->v.z;` gives the target's `ldr r2`/`ldr r1` pair. Our code then loads `z` above the two stores, and the target loads it after them. With a `const` pointer the registers swap back. An `s64` copy of `x`/`y` keeps `z` after the stores but uses the pair `r2`/`r3`.
  - `func_ov013_022001b8` (3 to 2, `d/01b8/v_xb1BOOL.cpp`): `if (mode == 1) { m = TRUE; b = 0x1000; } else { m = FALSE; b = 0x1000; }` (the round ovhi19 constant-in-both-arms trick) moves the `0x1000` out of the constant pool order. The target keeps both `movne r3, #0` and `moveq r3, #1`, and in every arm order and condition sense we tried, one of the two `mov`s is hoisted above the `cmp`. A `switch` gave the same result.
  - These did not move: `func_ov002_021e2690` (2): two-definition `t`/`m` for the `8`/`3` stores at every position, and a two-definition loop index with `for` or `do`. `func_ov003_021e1204` (2): `switch` on the kind (9), every type for `k` or a `v` copy, and two-definition `e`/`k`. `func_ov003_021ead50` (2): `Set` returning `BOOL` in four shapes. No matched object in the build has `cmp; strne; beq`, so there is no known source pattern for that sequence. `func_ov015_02211b2c` (2): name, index or anim-pointer locals at every position, and two-definition forms for each argument. `func_ov000_021be5dc` (3): `s64` accumulators, `z` at function scope, and two-definition `z`. `func_ov000_02153804` (17), `func_ov015_021fce94` (13) and `func_ov010_0220640c` (6): `tdc.py`, two-definition `sx`/`sy`/`t`, and a two-definition `dirty` value inside `Ov015Cam_Init` (139 or worse, because the `if` blocks split the inlined code).
- **Tools.** `scratchpad/ovhi20/t/tdc.py` (literal to two-definition variable, at every position), `t/tdv.py` (adds a second definition after each simple assignment; `TDV_SPAN=n` also tries the next `n` lines) and `t/vr.py base func module varfile` (runs a list of `(name, [(old, new)])` variants in parallel and writes `v_<name>` next to the base file).
## Sweeping pool words for table entries, and block-local swap-loop indexes (all overlays, round big14)

No new matches this round. Drafts are in `scratchpad/big14/d/` and `scratchpad/big14/best/`.

- **The table-entry sweep found no new cases.** `scratchpad/big14/t/tblsweep.py ROOT` disassembles every function that is below 100% in `build/usa/report.json`. It reads each `ldr rX, [pc, #n]` pool word that points into `0x02000000..0x02400000`. It prints the words that land inside a data symbol (own overlay first, then main) at a non-zero offset, and the words that land inside a named data symbol (not `data_`/`func_`) that has a `data_` symbol in between. `t/uses.py ROOT module pooladdr` shows the instructions that use one pool word. The hits (`scratchpad/big14/sweep_raw*.txt`):

These are the constant angles `0x3ff0`/`0x7ff0`/`0xbff0` (index `0x3ff`/`0x7ff`/`0xbff`). Every draft of these four already writes `FX_SinCosTable_[(0x3fff >> 4) * 2]` or `FX_SINCOS(0x3ff * 2)`.

The drafts already index these as arrays.
