## TU1 blockers, round 5: where the zero copy comes from (main, round mainpool10)

Nothing matched this round, so TU1 is still not merged. Drafts are in `scratchpad/mainpool10/d/`. The test harness `scratchpad/mainpool10/v/t524.sh 'body'` compiles a small standalone C copy of `func_0204f524` (`d/f524_t.c`, `BODY` replaced by the argument) in about 0.3 s, which is much faster than the `TextWin` drafts for sweeps.

- **The hoisted zero becomes `mov r4, r5` only when `i = 0` is in the block the zero is hoisted into.** The backend replaces a hoisted `mov rX, #0` with a copy of a register that it knows holds 0. That knowledge passes down a chain of single-predecessor blocks, but not into a block with two predecessors, even when both of them leave the register at 0 (`if (c) { ...; i = 0; }` before the loop changes nothing). The join block after the never-true `if` is the loop preheader, so the `ISDU` form of round mainpool8 always gives `mov r4, #0`. Every form with the copy has `i = 0` after the `if`, or has no `if` at all (the plain constant form also has the copy, but with constant ordering). The same knowledge is what lets `func_0204ffdc` use `sb` for every later zero. There it works across joins because the register is assigned only once in the function.
- **Where the base `mov`s end up.** The backend moves the base definitions down to the end of their block. So with `D I S U` (definitions first, then `i = 0`, the store and the `if`), the `strb` still comes out before the two `mov`s. With `D U I S` the definitions and the store are in different blocks. After the `if` is removed, the merged block is scheduled in source order apart from critical-path ties. `mov r5, #0` goes first (it feeds both `strb` and the copy), and the two `mov`s come out before `strb` (3 diffs).
- **Copies of two-definition variables are not propagated.** This gives forms with the copy and the right registers, but the schedule is still wrong:
  - `d/f524_3_basecopy.c` (3): `u8 *ba, *bc` get the two definitions (`ba = A; bc = B; if ((u32)win & 3) {...}`) at the top, and then `i = 0; win->numBlocks = i; a = ba; c = bc;`. It has `mov r4, r5` and the target's registers. Only `strb` is after the two `mov`s.
  - `d/f524_8_zcopy.c` (8) has exactly the target's instructions, but with the registers rotated. `n` gets the two definitions and is stored (`n = 0; win->numBlocks = n; ... if (...) { n = 0; ... } i = n; z = n;`). The loop uses `u16 z` for all three zeros and is a `do`/`while`, because a `for` loop keeps its entry test once `i` is no longer a known constant. Variables joined by a copy share one register, and the group ranks at the later declaration of its members. In 5760 type, declaration and copy orders (`v/g7.py`), the register with the zeros never got `r4`. When the copy got `r4`, it was `i`, and `z` held the zeros. The best is the rotation at 8.
- **Hoisted conversion temporaries.** When `z` is `u32` and still used in the loop, the hoisted `(u16)z` for the `strh` gets `r4`, the lowest register (36: `mov r4, r8` plus an extra `r8`). When every use goes through the conversion, it is merged into `z` and ranks last again.
- **Tried without success:** `if`/`else` with the definitions in different orders (the `tst` stays), `?:` forms (folded), a `goto` from the `if` body into the loop (the branch stays), unconditional or conditional redefinitions at the end of the loop body (kept as copies through scratch registers), `asm { mov a, #... }` (this turns off optimization for the whole function: `i` is loaded from the pool and the loop test comes back), absolute-address objects (`u8 objVram[4] : 0x6400000;` compiles, but gives constant ordering), a `volatile` store, and narrow loop-index types.
- **`func_0204ffdc`.** `dy` behaves like the zero register of round mainpool8: a two-definition `dy` defined far from its `if` keeps the `tst` (the backend only removes the `if` when the definition is in the same block chain). Next to the definition, it breaks the zero copies in the blocks after the join (`busy` gets `mov #0`). `long`/`s64`/`u32`/`int dy` alone all stay at 52.
- **`func_020508f4` and `func_02051a34`.** A two-definition `fn` (`if ((u32)win & 3) fn = font->...;`) keeps its load and branch (the backend does not know the loaded value). A copy through a second local is folded away. In `1a34` the `0xffff0000` mask also gives the `-1` of `win->args[0] != -1` (`cmp r0, r6, asr #16`), so a mask variable would also need that compare rewritten.
## `const` source pointers, a `volatile` stack parameter and the ITCM padding (itcm, round itcm3)

`func_01ffc318`, `func_01ffc624` and `MIi_DmaSetParameters` (was `func_01ff8020`) matched this round. Drafts are in `scratchpad/itcm3/`.

- **`const` on a source pointer gives "load all, then store all" with descending registers.** `func_01ffc318` (point in a rotated box) copies the translation into the matrix: the target loads `trans->x/y/z` into `r4`/`r3`/`r2` and stores them after the `MTX_Inverse43` arguments are set up. Every order of locals, loads and stores (216 forms), struct copies, pointer locals, inlines and two-definition variables gave either an `ldm` or the stores in the wrong order. Declaring the parameter `const VecFx32 *trans` and writing the three plain `m._30 = trans->x;` assignments matches. A matched main-module struct copy with the same shape (`obj->pos = *pos` in `func_0206500c`, which also takes a `const` pointer) gave the hint: `scratchpad/itcm3/t/pat.py` searches all modules for `ldr rN; ldr rN-1; ldr rN-2` from one base followed by stores of the same registers. When the target has descending registers for three loads from one pointer, try `const` on that pointer first.
- **`const` on an inline's parameter adds a register copy.** In the same function the box test reads `v.x` into `r1`, compares it, and copies it to `r2` for the second compare (`mov r2, r1`). `HitInBox(const VecFx32 *p, ...)` does exactly this; without `const` there is no copy. The `MathItcm.h` helpers (`HitInBox`, `HitInCylinder`, `HitInCube`, `HitMagSq`, `HitMagSqXZ`) now take `const` pointers; the functions that already used them still match.
- **A `volatile` stack parameter copied into a local: `func_01ffc624` matched.** It is `func_01ffc318` with `HitInCylinder` and `HitMagSqXZ` at the end. The `const` changes took it from 29 to 5: the `x` copy was right, but the target loads the stack parameter `h` right after the first compare (`cmp r1, r4; ldr r3, [sp, #0x90]`), not at its use three blocks later. The fix has two parts. The parameter is `volatile fx32 h`, so the inline's parameter copy is a real read where the inline starts (as in round lo12, but here to load early, not late). And `HitInCylinder` names its bounds, `BOOL ret = TRUE; fx32 nr = -r; fx32 hh = h;`, in that order; without the locals the read is still propagated to its use, and with `hh` declared before `nr` their registers swap. `func_01ffc4ec` and `func_01ffc584` use the same inline and still match. A two-definition `hh` (`if ((u32)r & 3) hh = h;`) also moved the load, but the scheduler put it before the `cmp` (4 diffs). `register`, `const` or other integer types on `h`, the inline's parameter order, a wrapper inline, a struct parameter for `r`/`h`, nested range inlines, `&&` or early-return forms and 1,000 permuter iterations did not help.
- **The 32 zero bytes at the start of ITCM.** Nothing refers to `0x01ff8000`..`0x01ff8020`. While `func_01ff8020` was unmatched they were part of its gap object; as a gap of their own, the linker dead-strips them and everything after moves down by 0x20. `mi_dma_itcm.c` now starts with a `#pragma force_active on` asm block of eight `andeq r0, r0, r0` (the zero word), named `ITCMi_Pad` and listed as `kind:data(any)` in `symbols.txt`, because `dsd` refuses to analyse it as a function. Written as `dcd 0` it links the same, but the report shows it at 40%.
- **`MIi_DmaSetParameters` matched on the first try** (TwlSDK, `libs/nitro/mi/mi_dma_itcm.c`). Its mode bits are 1 = no interrupt disable, 2 = the dummy reads and DMA0 clear, 4 = skip the clear, 0x10/0x20 = 32/16-bit fill source. The names in `mi_dma.c` (`NOINT = 2`, `WAIT = 4`) do not follow this.
- **`func_01ffae08` (13), `func_01ff868c` (36), `func_01ff9f9c` / `func_01ff9060`: unchanged.** `const` pointer locals and `const` copies of the subtract/scale inlines, `s64` accumulators for the distance, struct copies of the scaled position, and `tdv.py` over every assignment did not move the two `ae08` spots.

## Main-module triage, a field cached in a local, and the TWL blocker (round mainmid1)

- **Triage.** The full table of unmatched main functions outside the TU1/TU2 sets is in `scratchpad/mainmid1/triage.md`. About 25 small main functions can't be matched yet because at least one `bl` goes into TWL-only code: its reloc in `relocs.txt` is `module:none`. Examples are `func_0200c0c4`, `func_020163b8`, `func_0200d85c` and `func_02049344`; the table has the full list. Most of the rest are library code (SDK, NNS, MSL) or register-allocation near-misses.
- **`func_02039844` matched (`GfxMgr.cpp`).** Two changes were needed:
  - **The `0x20` register.** It shared a register with the loop index. Building it in the reused index variable with the two-definition trick fixed that: `i = 0x20; if ((u32)pool & 3) i = 0x20; align = i << pool->alignShift;`.
  - **The load order.** The target loads `numBlocks` (`[r1, #8]`) before `alignShift` (`[r1]`). Ours got this order when `count = pool->numBlocks;` comes right before that line and `count` is used only in the loop condition. The later `i == pool->numBlocks` must still read the field: reusing `count` there too, or moving the read to the top of the function, gives a different register or 64 differences.
- **`func_0203b12c` (1 → 13): mul operand order.** The target's `mul r3, r2, r5` (`bpp * db`) comes out when the product is written in both of its uses: `dst = buf + (((u16)bpp * db) >> 3); db = ((u16)bpp * db) & 7;`. That moves the registers around (23). A climb over the declaration order (`declhc.py`) brings it back to 13, and now only `db` and `b` are swapped in the inner loop. These did not help: the types of `b` and `db`, `b` declared in the inner block, and a `7 - db` local.
- **`func_0206d43c` (28 → 14).** Write the align inline as `u32 m = total - 1; x += m; return x & ~m;`. Keep `dataSize` in a local `d` and use `s->dataSize * s->slotCount + d`, so the compiler doesn't factor `a*b + a` into `a*(b+1)`. The rest is registers. It still has to share a file with `func_0206d99c` (the `CODASAVE` pool).
- **Unchanged.**
  - `func_02041080` (7). A two-definition `z = 0` before `if (load)` gives the target's structure, but `z` lands in `r0` instead of `r2` (11).
  - `func_0203d298` (7). Moving `prev = pad->prev;` before the `0x30` fixup gives 8 → 7.
  - `WH_ChangeSysState` (`02046070`, `020459cc`), `func_0206e858`, `func_02049690` and `func_0206f6d4`: no new form helped.
- **Tools.** In `scratchpad/mainmid1/`:
  - `declhc.py src func N` hill-climbs the order of the top declaration block.
  - `var.py base func 'old' variants.txt` scores text replacements (variants separated by `====`).
  - `sd.py` and `sdr.py` give the aligned diff count, raw and register-normalised.

## Two-definition sweep over every near-miss, and arms that stay predicated (all overlays, round ovhi21)

This round scored every draft of every unmatched overlay function below `0x500` bytes (four newest drafts each, 541 compiles) and ran `tdc.py` (literal as a two-definition variable, `TDNEAR=6`) and `tdv.py` (second definition after each assignment, `TDV_SPAN=1`) on the best draft of all 44 functions at 2 to 10 diffs, then `tdv2.py` (below) on the 37 that were left. Eight functions reached 0, and seven of them went into the build. Drafts are in `scratchpad/ovhi21/d/auto/<func>/` (`base.*` is the starting draft, `*.tdc`/`*.tdv`/`*.tdv2` the best variant).

- **Matched by `tdc.py`, a constant made into a two-definition variable just before its use:**
  - `func_ov006_021cd914` (2 to 0): the `u32 idx = 0xffff` that a `switch` then overwrites. `noIdx = 0xffff; if ((u32)type & 3) { noIdx = 0xffff; } ... u32 idx = noIdx;` at the top of `case 7` puts `mov r7, #0xff` after the pool load, as in the target. The condition here is on an `int` parameter, so it can be true at run time. That is harmless because the second definition stores the same value.
  - `func_ov006_021e9f80` (2 to 0): the `0x8000` busy mask of the `SQRTCNT` poll loop, defined right before the `s64` sum. This moves `ldr r2, [r5]` below the first `smull`.
  - `func_ov014_02202a34` (4 to 0): the `0x100` in `(u8 *)self + 0x100`, passed as the pointer argument of `func_ov003_021d85c8`. With `(u8 *)self + off`, the `add r0` and the stack-argument `str` move to the target's places.
  - `func_ov003_021eb818` (8 to 0): the `1` stored to `data_ov000_021ccd54->unk_ac` at the end of the `if` arm. It has to use struct-copy versions of the `PartA`/`PartB` copy inlines (`*d = *s`). The field-by-field versions that its neighbours `021eba54`/`021ebae4` need give 8 diffs here. Both pairs are now in `ov003_021eba54.cpp` (`Ov003PartA_Assign` and `Ov003PartA_Copy`).
  - `func_ov015_022078bc` (9 to 0) matches with `self + kq` (`kq = 0xcb8`) for `func_ov000_02191b70`, but it is not in the build: it uses real literals from the ov015 pool `0x02212ccc..0x02212d2f`. That pool also holds the strings of the pointer tables at `0x02212cac..0x02212ccb` and two strings of `func_ov015_02207adc`, and contains `"mc/c1rob4"` and `"c1rob4cha"` twice. With `extern` arrays it gives 103 diffs.
- **Matched by `tdv.py`:** `func_ov015_0220ae90` (5 to 0) needs `if ((u32)self & 3) { dec = FLD(s32, p, 0xe0); }` after the declarations. `tdc.py` also found a form with the `0` of `st->unk_1c != 0`.
- **A second definition inside one arm keeps both arms predicated.** `func_ov003_021ddce4` (3 to 0, found by `tdv.py`): the target clears two bits with `biceq ... biceq` in one arm and sets them with `bicne; orrne ...` in the other. MWCC hoisted the common first `bic r0, r0, #1` out of both arms. `if ((u32)self & 3) { p = self->unk_44; }` as the first statement of the `then` arm stops that. The same works for `func_ov013_022001b8` (3 to 0), which needs `movne r3, #0; moveq r3, #1` for a `mode == 1` argument (round ovhi19). Every ternary and bool form gave `mov r3, #1; cmp; movne r3, #0`. This one matches:

  ```c
  BOOL m;
  if (FLD(u8, self, 0x15d) != 1) {
      m = FALSE;
      if ((u32)self & 3) {
          m = FALSE;
      }
  } else {
      m = TRUE;
  }
  func_ov013_02202dc8(self, 0xa00, 0x1000, m, 0);
  ```

  A second definition of `m`, or of a `b = 0x1000` set in both arms, works in either arm when it comes after the arm's own assignment. A second definition of an `Ov013Actor *s = self` copy that is passed to the call works anywhere in either arm. All of these need the `!= 1` condition. With `== 1`, the best is 2. When the target has a pair of predicated instructions where ours has one unconditional instruction before the `cmp`, put a second definition into one arm.
- **`tdv2.py`** puts the second definition at every later statement of the function, at any block depth (`TDV_MAXD` lines, default 12), not only right after the assignment. Since the condition is never true for an aligned pointer, any position keeps the behaviour. `TDV_IN=<inline>` (also for `tdv.py`, and `TDC_IN` for `tdc.py`) runs the search inside an inline that the function uses. On its own, it found no match this round.
- **Smaller results (not matched):**
  - `func_ov000_0217a1f8` (3 to 2): `kq = 0x78` with two definitions before `func_ov000_0217a328((u8 *)self, kq, -1)` gives the target's `mvn r2, #0`. Now only `mov r1, #0x78` is scheduled before `mov r0, r4`. A two-definition `-1` instead puts `mvn r2` first. Wrapper inlines, a two-definition `self` copy and other types for `kq` stay at 2.
  - `func_ov000_0218a5ec` (4 to 2): a two-definition `k = 0x8000` for the stack argument, with the `to`/`from` loads in locals `a`/`b` (order `t, k, a, b`, then the `if`), gives the target's `ip` register and the store last. `mov ip, #0x8000` is still scheduled after the two `ldrh`.
  - `func_ov002_021e2690` (2): storing `0x34` before `0x30`, as the target does, swaps the hoisted `8`/`3` registers (4). Two-definition `8`/`3` before the loop, at the top, or in the loop body (`tdc.py`) are all worse.
  - `func_ov003_021ead50` (2) and `func_ov000_0219badc` (5): no matched object in the build has `cmp; <op>ne; beq` (checked with `llvm-objdump` over all 2015 objects). The only `<op>ne` followed by an `eq` branch is `cmpne`, which comes from `&&`/`||` chains. Second definitions in either arm of the two `if (t)` forms did not help.
  - `func_ov003_021d883c` (2): a two-definition `d` pointer, a struct copy and copy inlines all load `z` above the two stores.
  - These did not move: `021e1204`, `02211b2c`, `021be5dc` (also the `ToDepth`/`CalcDepth` forms and `TDC_IN=CalcDepth`), `021e1bb4` (`tdv`/`tdc`/`tdv2` inside `ov006_Caption_Draw`), `0220640c` (two-definition `0`/`1` arguments of `func_0203cb78` at every position of case 0), `021e51f8` (the `1` passed to `Ov006_Begin` goes to `r0` instead of `r1` with any inline parameter type, a `menu` local or a two-definition argument), `02201bf4` and `021d5b98`/`021d5e68` (string pools).

## Two-definition locals on mid-size overlay drafts (overlays under 0x500 bytes, round ovmid1)

The list was every unmatched overlay function under 0x500 bytes whose best draft had more than 10 diffs. That was 168 candidates; 145 had drafts and 78 were left after removing string-pool, dsprot/TWL and `__sinit` functions. The batch ran `tdc.py` (with `TDNEAR=5`) and then `tdv.py` on 47 of them, and a statement-run climb (`runs.py` plus `hcm.py`) on 30. Drafts are in `scratchpad/ovmid1/best/` and `scratchpad/ovmid1/b/<addr>/`.

- **Matched: `func_ov011_021fd4d8`** (added to `src/ov011/ov011_021fd4a4.cpp`). The base draft had 16 diffs. A two-definition `kq = 0x20; if ((u32)self & 3) { kq = 0x20; }` used in `self + kq` took it to 10. An hcm climb over the order of the 0x1d4 to 0x1ec field stores took it to 0.
- **`func_ov002_021ec010` (22 to 2):** a two-definition `s16 z = 0`, used for every zero store. The `-1` store is written `z - 1`. What is left is the order of `mov r4, r0` and `ldr r5, [r1]`, which 15 minutes of the permuter did not fix.
- **`func_ov011_021fff38` (19 to 7):** a two-definition pointer to `data_ov011_02207258`, placed after the stack copy `p = *(Ov011Vec *)(self->unk_48 + 0x68);` in case 2, plus explicit `t.y`, `t.z`, `t.x` stores before the `home` declaration.
- **`func_ov009_021ff908` (38 to 9):** a two-definition `s32 z = 0` placed before `v = self->unk_b8;`, then passed as both zero arguments of `func_ov000_021987b8`.
- **Smaller gains:**
  - **`func_ov002_021d8d00` (24 to 13):** `s32 chr` instead of `s16`, read before `val`, and `y`/`x` assigned after the clamp.
  - **`func_ov011_021fb02c` (13 to 6):** `dx`/`dy`/`dz` declared before the entity pointer.
  - **`func_ov015_02201344` (154 to 29):** a two-definition `kq = 0x30`.
  - **`0217ea30`:** 70 to 33.
  - **`021bb8dc`:** 100 to 62.
  - **`021d6bac`:** 75 to 62.
  - **`021c6d64`:** 19 to 11.
  - **`021daae4`:** 30 to 26, from moving `v.y -= b->y`.
- **Tools:**
  - `tdc.py` takes `TDLIT`, which limits the literals it tries.
  - `fx.py` applies replacements inside one function only. Plain `vr.py` replacements also hit other functions in the same file.
  - `runs.py` turns each run of three or more simple statements into an hcm block.
  - `batch.sh` and `batch3.sh` run these tools over a list.
- **Stuck:**
  - **`021ffd0c` / `021ffe8c` (13, ov010):** in the target, the 6-byte `Ov010Rot` copy keeps the table base in `r3` and loads `z` after the vector `ldm`/`stm`. These all failed:

    - two-definition bases

    - pointer copies
  - **`021daae4`:** `def` and `vp` have swapped stack slots compared with the target, and the target reads `v` straight from the stack. Declaring `vp` first, or using a plain `Ov003Vec` local, gives 86 to 104.

## Overlay string-pool merges (all overlays, round ovpool1)

- **`func_ov015_022078bc` is in the build.** It is now the first function of `src/ov015/ov015_022078bc.cpp` (the old `ov015_02207adc.cpp`, renamed), which covers `.text 0x022078bc..0x02208070` and `.data 0x02212d08..0x02212d30` under `#pragma pool_strings on` / `dont_reuse_strings off`. `strrefs.py --claim 0x02212d08 0x02212d30` made the pool `@stringBase0`. `func_ov015_02207adc` now uses the literals `"ang00"`/`"ang05"`.
- **`strrefs.py` can join two pools.** It shows `0x02212ccc..0x02212d2f` as one pool, but `"mc/c2rob2"` ends exactly on the 4-byte boundary `0x02212d08`, so a new object can start there. The file's own strings start at `0x02212d08` (`"c1rob4"`, `"mc/c1rob4"`, `"c1rob4cha"`, `"ang00"`, `"ang05"`). The part before that (`0x02212ccc..0x02212d07`) holds the strings of the pointer tables at `0x02212cac..0x02212ccb`, and it repeats `"c1rob4cha"` and `"mc/c1rob4"`. With string reuse on, one file can't hold the same string twice, so the tables and their strings are a different file. When a pool from `--pools` has a string that ends on a 4-byte boundary, check whether the strings after it belong to different code. The tables are still unclaimed.
- **Literal arguments go into the pool last argument first.** The first build matched every function but failed the ROM check: the pool started `"mc/c1rob4"`, `"c1rob4"`. `match.py` hides this, because it ignores relocation targets. The inline was `Ov015_SetAnim(o, name, file)`, called as `(self, "c1rob4", "mc/c1rob4")`, and its body copies `file` first. Swapping the parameters to `(o, file, name)`, called as `(self, "mc/c1rob4", "c1rob4")`, gave the same code and the target's pool order. So MWCC pools a call's literal arguments right to left, whatever order the inlined body uses them in. The reversed `Ov003Big_Load(self, ext, name)` in `func_ov003_021e44dc` fits the same rule. If the ROM check fails after a pool claim, `cmp` the built overlay with the extracted one and look at the order of the pool's strings.
- **Still blocked:**
  - **ov003 pool `0x021f8564..0x021f85c1`.** It is used from `func_ov003_021e1034` (in `ov003_021e0e8c.c`) to `func_ov003_021e6fb4` (in `ov003_021e6cb8.cpp`). One file would have to cover `.text 0x021e0e8c..0x021e7274`: about 14 files, a mix of `.c` and `.cpp`, plus three unmatched functions. These are `021e1204` (2, the `ldrh` into `r1` instead of `r0`; `switch` forms gave 27, and `k` types and casts gave 2), `021e44dc` (3 with `scratchpad/ovhi18/d/44dc/v_rev.cpp`: the target loads `"g1plt01e"` into `r6` before the first `func_ov000_0218f074` call, and ours loads it after) and `021e6274` (14). For `021e44dc`, a pool with other strings before these two still gave 3. Locals, a two-definition `ext` or `name`, split inlines and an inline parameter for the `1` arguments gave 3 to 39.
  - **ov006 pool `0x021ebbb0..0x021ebc65`.** `func_ov006_021d3f04` matches with literals (`scratchpad/ovlow2/d3f04.cpp`). The pool is also used by `021cec68` (best draft 40, `scratchpad/ovlo5b/d/cec68b.cpp`), `021cf420` (59, `scratchpad/ovlow1/cf420.cpp`) and `021d4118` (33, `scratchpad/ovlo3/d/d4118_b.cpp`). Its first string `"--------------------"` is loaded from the `.data` word at `0x021ebbac`, so that word is part of the file too.
  - `scratchpad/ovpool1/unmatched_pool_users.txt` lists every overlay pool loaded by a function that is not in any `delinks.txt` (64 rows). The ones above are the only ones with a draft known to match once it has the pool.
## Points-to through one global array, and a reused parameter as the stack-pop variable (itcm, round itcm4)

`func_01ffae08` (ray against an unsorted triangle mesh) and `func_01ff868c` (the same ray test over a quadtree) matched this round, as `src/Main/Hit/HitTriItcm.cpp` and `src/Main/Hit/HitTriTreeItcm.cpp`. Their shared types, the attribute-filter and edge macros and the small inlines are in `include/Main/Hit/HitTriItcm.h`. Drafts are in `scratchpad/itcm4/`.

- **Both hit-record pointers must point into the same global array.** The two old `ae08` spots (the hit position stored as `y` then `z`, and `origin - info->pos` loading `pos.x`/`pos.y` before the first store to `e`) had one cause. The record pointer was `info = &data_02fe14b4` (overflow slot) or `&data_02fe0f50[n]`. `data_02fe14b4` is really `&data_02fe0f50[23]` (`0x564 = 23 * 0x3c`). Written that way, MWCC knows that `info` points into `data_02fe0f50` and can't alias the address-taken stack vector `e`. So the `info->pos` loads are no longer held behind the stores to `e`, which matches the target's schedule. With `info = &data_02fe0f50[23]` the function went from 13 to 2. A test that read `data_02fe14b4.pos` directly also gave the target's schedule, which showed that this was an alias problem and not register order. The last 2 (the `y`/`z` store order) then matched with plain `HitVecScale(&p, owner->scale, &info->pos)`. Before the alias fix, that form gave `stmib`. Look for this when the target hoists loads through a pointer above stores to an escaped local, and the pointer is chosen between two globals: one of them may be an element of the other.
- **`func_01ff868c`: the popped node is the `root` parameter.** With the `ae08` body, the quadtree walk was 23 (sequence) off: the target stores the decremented `top` before loading `stack[top]`, keeps the node in the index register (`ldr r1, [r0, r1, lsl #2]`) and reloads `top` for the first push's hoisted `top * 4`. Pop and push spellings (`top--;` first, `stack[top] = x; top++;`, `top -= 1`, casts, `volatile` or address-taken `top`, inlines taking `&top`, `while`/`for`/`else` loop forms, push loops, 155 declaration orders of `node` and `top`) gave 21–145. A focused permuter run (`PERM_RANDOMIZE` around the pop, bounds test and pushes) found it in 192 iterations: there is no `node` local. The loop reuses the parameter, `root = stack[--top];`. In the source the parameter is now named `node`. Before declaring a fresh local for a loop's "current" pointer, try reusing a parameter that is dead after its first use.
- **Focused permuter runs are fast.** With `PERM_RANDOMIZE( ... )` around just the statements that differ (balanced braces, inside the loop), the 0x9d4-byte function ran at about 4 iterations a second, against about 3 a second for a whole-function run that found nothing in 15 minutes. `scratchpad/itcm4/prun.sh` is `itcm3/prun.sh` pointed at this clone.
- **`const` sweep (part 1).** `scratchpad/itcm4/t/pat2.py` is `itcm3/t/pat.py` limited to the ARM functions that `report.json` lists as unmatched, with the overlay bases read from `arm9_overlays/overlays.yaml`. The old script read none of the overlays, because there is no `.yaml` beside each `.bin`. It finds 12 functions where the target loads three words from one base into descending registers and then stores them in order: main `0205e29c`; ov000 `02189b44`, `0219d9fc`, `021a0d4c`, `021aa978`, `021b4130`, `021bd8ac`; ov009 `02208b24`; ov011 `021fc0b8`, `02203ef0`; ov014 `0220a67c`, `022153e0`. In every best draft that was checked, these spots already compile to the same pattern (plain struct copies). The remaining diffs are elsewhere, so `const` had nothing to fix. `021b4130` has no draft. Variants with any offsets (`pat3.py`) or halfwords (`pat4.py`) found nothing new.
- **`func_01ff9f9c` (154 masked) and `func_01ff9060` (320 to 312 masked): not matched.** The new tricks barely move them. In `9060` (regenerated in `scratchpad/itcm4/9060/g3.cpp`), reusing `root`, `&data_02fe0f50[23]` and `HitVecScale` save 8 masked diffs. In `9f9c` the record-pointer change does nothing. The real blocker is the constants. The target sets a zero in `r3` at entry, uses it as the high word of every rounding add (`adc rX, rY, r3`, with `0x800` in a register too), and builds the edge threshold as `sub r0, r3, #5`, which it spills once for all three edges. `ae08` spills the threshold the same way but builds it with `mvn r0, #4`. A `zero` local in the threshold (`< zero - 5`, initialised at entry or at its declaration), `found = 0` at entry (frame 0x11c, closer to the target's 0x118 but `found` is spilled) and `x + 5 < 0` (worse) did not reproduce it. About 450 iterations of whole-function permuter on `9f9c` (base score 13295, best 7775) found only noise, such as moving one edge subtraction into a function-scope temporary.
## A preheader constant, a global re-read in a loop, and main near-misses (round mainmid2)

`func_02041080` (`PlttRes_2.cpp`) and `func_02049e20` (`SndMgr_5.cpp`) matched this round. Drafts are in `scratchpad/mainmid2/d/` and `scratchpad/mainmid2/nm/`.

- **`func_02041080`: a constant in the loop preheader.** The target sets the `1` of `bit << j` once, after the `j < count` guard and before the loop. Write the loop as `j = 0; if (j < count) { bit = 1; if ((u32)res & 3) bit = 1; do { ... } while (++j < count); }`, with `u16 bit;` declared first. A plain `for` puts the constant before the guard, or rebuilds it in the body.
- **`func_02049e20`: a global read again in every iteration.** The target loads `data_ov000_021ccd54` again inside the search loop. Two changes were needed:
  - Pass `&data_ov000_021ccd54->a` to a small pointer inline (`SndMgr_IsAreaAt`).
  - Copy the table entry's bytes into locals (`u8 a = e->a; ...`) before the call.
  
  Without these, MWCC hoists `listener->a` out of the loop. The second parameter is `const SndVec *pos`. `VEC_Distance` now takes `const` pointers.
- **Near-misses that improved:**
  - **`func_02064b54` (28 → 20).** An `if`/`else` with a `VEC_Add` in each branch gives the target's tail-merged call (`beq; b; add r1`).
  - **`func_02063650` (17 → 2).** A two-definition global address: `gc = &data_02092844; if ((u32)self & 3) gc = ...;`. Only the scheduling of one `ldr` is left.
  - **`func_0206f6d4` (22 → 15).** Use `int item = k + 0x7000;` and pass it to an inline that takes a `u16`.
  - **`func_0206d99c` (70 → 40), drafted with `func_0206d43c` in one file (they share the `CODASAVE` pool).** MWCC folds `(x - a) + a`. Writing one side as `+ (s->slotSize + sizeof(u32))` keeps both the `sub` and the `add`. The align-down is an inline.
  - **`func_02042f40` (18 → 8).** Only with a hacky duplicate-call form found by `tdv.py`.
- **`func_0203c2bc` (never drafted before).** The draft is `d/c2bc/a.cpp`, appended to `GfxSys_4.cpp`. It uses inlines for the texture and palette VRAM setup and for the heap init. Every instruction is right after register normalisation (4 differences), but `sys` gets `sb` instead of `r4` (116 raw). `tdc.py` found that a two-definition `0x80` before `func_02015510` splits the block and gives `sys` `r4` (74 raw), but adds other differences. Placing it would move the end of `GfxSys_4.cpp` to `0x0203c638`.
- **Unchanged or blocked:**
  - **`func_0204a3b8` is already matched** (`SndMgr_6.cpp`, under `SNDMGR_DELINK_0204A3B8`). It can't be placed until the TWL gap after it is resolved.
  - **`func_0206e858`.** The target loads the pool word for `data_0214b105` twice. `data_0214b104.name` followed by `data_0214b105` gives the right sequence, but 4 pool words instead of 3.
  - **`func_02015510` (19).** The switch tree uses a different pivot (target `0x80`) and drops case 0.
  - These are still a few register or scheduling differences:
    - `func_0203b12c` (1)
    - `func_0203d298` (7)
    - `func_0206d43c` (14)
    - `func_02049690` (10)
    - `func_0203a984` (10)
    - `func_0206e3a0` (2)
  - `func_020429a0` (a large 3D OAM builder) and `func_02024730` (NNS cell-to-OAM library code) were not drafted.
- **Tools.** In `scratchpad/mainmid2/`:

## ov003 and ov006 pool blockers (round ovpool2)

Neither pool could be merged this round: `func_ov003_021e1204` and `func_ov003_021e6274` still differ, and the three ov006 blockers are far from matching. Drafts are in `scratchpad/ovpool2/best/`.

- **`func_ov003_021e44dc` matches with real literals (3 to 0, `best/44dc_match_literals.cpp`).** Drop the `Ov003Big_Load` inline. Load `name` into a local before the first call, and assign `ext` with a plain statement after that call:

  ```c
  const char *name = "gm/g1plt01";
  const char *ext;
  func_ov000_0218f074(&self->res, (char *)name, 1, 0, 0, 1);
  ext = "g1plt01e";
  self->res = 0;
  STD_CopyString(self->unk_290, name);
  STD_CopyString(self->unk_2b0, ext);
  ```

  An inline parameter, or an initialised local, is loaded at the top of the function. A plain assignment after the call is loaded after it, as in the target. The literals appear in pool order (`name` first). A two-definition `ext` gives 16 to 47 at every position. With `extern char[]` names instead of literals, this form has 7 diffs: the addresses are rebuilt at each use, not held in `r6`/`r7`. So this function can only go in with the pool merge.
- **`func_ov003_021e6274`: `n` must be `u8`, but then the decrement moves (14 to 8, `best/6274_u16mask_8.cpp`).** The target masks with `and #0xff`, so `n` is 8-bit. These remove every mask except one:
  - `u16 n` with `n = self->unk_18 & 0xff` and `n = (n - 1) & 0xff`;
  - `u32`/`s32` `n` with `(u8)` casts;
  - `s32 a` in the prototype of `func_ov000_02197dec`.

  The remaining diff is the `and r1, r5, #0xff` that MWCC emits before the 3-bit field store `self->unk_93_1 = n`. Any store expression other than a plain `u8` (`n & 7`, `(u16)n`, `n | 0`, an inline setter) keeps an instruction there. With `u8 n` the store has no extra instruction, but the new `and` of `n = n - 1` is scheduled above the store's `lsl`. That gives the new `n` its own register, which rotates `self` and the part pointer (46; 6 with registers ignored). These all kept that order:
  - `n--`, `n -= 1`, `--n` as the argument, and post-decrement in the store;
  - a separate `u8`/`s32` local for the old or the new value;
  - inlines for the store and for the animation call;
  - a re-read `self->unk_18` for the store;
  - storing through a `u8 *`.

  Two-definition locals around the store (`u8 m = n - 1; if ((u32)self & 3) m = n - 1; self->unk_93_1 = n; n = m;`, `best/6274_u8_twodef_struct2.cpp`) get the field insert into the target's order (2 with registers ignored), but the `and` still comes first. A barrier between the store and the decrement puts the whole store first (11). 40 minutes of the permuter found only invalid forms (stores moved into dead code after a `break`).
- **`func_ov003_021e1204` (2): unchanged.** The target loads `unk_16` into the entry pointer's register (`ldrh r1, [r1, #0x16]`), and every form we tried loads it into `r0`:
  - shift and mask expressions, divisions, and `s16`/`u32`/`s32` locals for the loaded value;
  - inlines taking the value, the entry, or the whole `if` block;
  - `const` entry pointers;
  - `u16`/`u32` bit-fields;
  - reading the entry again through `self->table`;
  - a live copy of `unk_06`;
  - a pointer local for `&self->unk_d0`;
  - every position and type of `k`;
  - compiling it as C++.

  The bit-field reads in matched code (`func_ov003_021cee68` and others) do load into the base register, but here a bit-field gave `r0` too. 25 minutes of the permuter found nothing.
- **ov006:**
  - `func_ov006_021d4118` (31 to 26, `best/d4118_26.cpp`): `tdc.py` made the `0xffff` in `self->unk4A != 0xffff` a two-definition variable, which fixes the load order of `data_ov006_021eb6fe[data_ov006_021ec884->unk5]`. What is left is the camera block. The target reads `rot.x`/`rot.y` before the two vector copies and `rot.z` after them. Declaration orders, store orders, by-value or `const` inlines and a non-`const` table did not change this.
  - `func_ov006_021cec68`: register rotations only. `tdc.py` with the `0x8b0` offset as a two-definition variable gave 44 to 38 (`best/cec68_38.cpp`).
  - `func_ov006_021cf420` (68): still structurally different (49 with registers ignored).
- **Tools.** `scratchpad/ovpool2/t/` has `tdc.py`, `tdv.py`, `tdv2.py`, `vr.py` and `hcm.py` pointed at this clone. `sdr.py` scores with registers normalised. Use it to see whether a variant changed the instruction order when the raw count stays the same: all of the 46-diff `u8` variants of `021e6274` look identical by raw count.

## Function-scope temporaries decide the zero register, and stack order from declarations (itcm, round itcm5)

`func_01ff9f9c` (sphere against one triangle) and `func_01ff9060` (the same test over a quadtree) matched this round, as `src/Main/Hit/HitTriSphereItcm.cpp`. `HitTriSphere` and the `elen[3]` edge lengths of `HitTri` are now in `HitTriItcm.h`. Drafts and tools are in `scratchpad/itcm5/`.

- **The "zero in `r3`" blocker was a live-range effect, not a constant spelling.** The target keeps the high word of the `0x800` rounding constant in `r3` from the entry (and `sub r0, r3, #5` for the edge threshold); our draft had the same zero register, but in `r2`, swapped with `-dist`. Two-definition zeros, `s64` rounding variables, a zero in the threshold and dot-product inlines all moved it the wrong way. The permuter found a nonsense `cx = cx;` in one edge's fail branch that put the zero in `r3`; that pointed to the real form: the cross-product results `cx`, `cy`, `cz` are **function-scope** locals (`fx32 cx, cy, cz;` after `found`), assigned in each of the three edge blocks, while `ex`/`ey`/`ez` stay block-scoped. With that, plain operand order matched everything through the edge tests (masked 154 to 3). When a register swap involves a value that is live through several similar blocks, try declaring the per-block temporaries once at function scope.
- **Operand order of the `(s64)` products is a strong lever on a wrong base.** Before the scope fix, a hill climb over the operand order of every product (`(s64)a * b`, `b * (s64)a`, cast on the other side; `t/gen2.py` and `t/climb2.py`) took the raw sequence diff from 691 to 214, with an irregular mix of orders per edge. All of that was compensation: once the scope was right, the plain uniform order was best. Treat an irregular "winning" operand pattern as a sign that something structural is still wrong.
- **The third segment test ends in `goto found`.** In both functions the target jumps straight to the hit code when `found != 0 && s > dist` after the third edge segment (jump-threaded `bgt found`), instead of a predicated `ldrne/cmpne`. Writing `if (found == 0 || s <= dist) { dist = s; p = c; } goto found;` inside the `HitMagSq <= radiusSq` block gives it; `found = 3` or a `goto found` inside the inner `if` keep the predicated form.
- **`&data_02fe0f50[23]` and `q->owner` read twice finished `9f9c`.** The last 14 diffs were the owner pointer: ours folded `owner + 8` for `MI_Copy48B` into a post-indexed `ldr r2, [r0], #8`. The round itcm4 alias fix (`info = &data_02fe0f50[23]` instead of `&data_02fe14b4`) plus `info->distSq = FX_Mul(FX_Mul(dist, block->mtx._00), q->owner->scale); MI_Copy48B(&q->owner->mtx, &m);` (no `owner` local) matches; either change alone does not.
- **Stack layout follows declaration order.** With the sphere body fixed, `9060` differed only in stack offsets (`p`/`e`/`m` at `0xc4`/`0xd0`/`0xdc` in the target). Declaring `MtxFx43 m; VecFx32 e; VecFx32 p;` in that order (later declarations get lower addresses here) matched; the other five orders gave 41 to 67 raw diffs. When only `[sp, #N]` offsets differ, permute the declarations of the address-taken locals.
- **Tools.** `t/mktmpl.py`/`t/mktmpl2.py` turn a draft into a template with `{{name|A|B}}` product, `[[name%%a%%b%%c]]` permutation and `<<name%%alt0%%alt1>>` alternative markers; `t/climb2.py` (greedy sweep) and `t/anneal.py` (random plateau walk) search them with `sd.py` raw and masked scores. `t/pat800.py` lists functions whose rounding uses a register for `0x800` or for the zero high word (only `9f9c` has both). `t/mc.sh` compiles a small reproduction and disassembles it with capstone (`t/odis.py`).

Moving a statement into or out of a bare block changes how MWCC schedules it, even when the statement does not use the block's variables.

## TU1 blockers, round 6: copies across joins, and a declaration climb on `0204e5d4` (main, round mainpool12)

Nothing matched this round, so TU1 is still not merged. Drafts and tools are in `scratchpad/mainpool12/` (`d/` drafts, `v/` sweeps; `v/t524.sh` is the round mainpool10 tester pointed at this clone).

- **`func_0204e5d4` (40 to 30).** `hc.py` over the 31 declarations (round mainpool5 ran it before the type scan) fixed the `bx` and `fn` spill slots. Draft `d/e5d4_30.cpp`. A second 1500-round climb and the type scan (`ts.py`) from there found nothing. Left: `pitch` and `src` swap spill slots (`0xc`/`0x2c`), and `bp`/`w`/`sh` rotate in the pixel loop. Block-scoping any subset of `bp`, `w`, `sh` and `t`, the order and operand order of the three pixel statements, a separate variable for the end-of-glyph `bp`, and a 200-round climb over the order of the six per-glyph setup statements all gave exactly 30. So the pixel-loop registers follow the global numbering (declarations), not that block.
- **The zero copy crosses a join when its source is a copy.** In the `0204f524` tester, `u16 z; ... z = i; if ((u32)win & 3) { a = ...; c = ...; z = i; }` (with `long i`, after `i = 0; win->numBlocks = i;`) and `z` for every zero in the loop gives the target's instructions, including `mov r4, r5`. Only the registers rotate (16: `i` r4, `a` r5, `c` r6, `z` r7; the target has `z` r4, `i` r5, `a` r6, `c` r7). With a `u32 z` the backend also hoists `mov r4, r8`, a copy of `z` across the join. A two-definition constant (`z = 0; if (...) z = 0;`) gives `mov r4, #0` instead. So the backend's "register known to hold 0" survives a join for a two-definition copy of a multi-definition `long`, but not for a two-definition constant. A single `z = i` is propagated as 0 (back to 3 diffs). About 2300 type, declaration, statement and loop-form samples of this form (`v/zs.py`, `v/zt.py`, `v/zr.py`) never ranked `z` below `i`. The same holds for the 1440 declaration and copy orders of the round mainpool10 `n`/`z` form (`v/zc.sh`, best 6: `c` and `z` swapped). The best are still the old 3 (`0204f524`) and 1 (`0204f5bc`). In `0204f5bc`, putting the definitions and the `if` before `if (win->page >= win->numPages)` gives the copy, but the two `mov`s stay above the `cmp` (4).
- **`func_02051a34` (35).** Moving `Model *m` to function scope changes the result a lot with its position (98 to 35), but no position beats 35, and a 600-round declaration climb with it found nothing. A two-definition mask variable (`s32 mk`, `win->args[0] != (mk >> 16)`, the `textPos` store written as `(w & mk) | (((u32)(u16)pos << 16) >> 16)`) gives the target's instructions, but `mk` gets `fp` (the target puts the mask in `r6`) and its `mov`/`rsb` move into the entry block (44). Defining `mk` in the inner loop's preheader needs the inner `while` written as `if (...) { ...; do { } while (...); }` with an `if (c == 0)` second definition. That puts the `mov`/`rsb` in the target's place, but it turns the outer loop into a guarded form (102). `if ((u32)win & 3)` there is hoisted out of the loop and stays.
- **`func_0204ffdc` (52).** `v/dyins.py` put `if ((u32)box & 3) { dy = 0; }` before every statement, with `s32` and `long dy` (about 260 compiles): none was below 52. `long dy` keeps `dy` as a variable everywhere, but `busy` then gets its own `mov #0`. Copies from multi-definition locals (`dx = 0; dy = dx; if (...) dy = dx;`, also with `i`, `alpha`, `t` and `long` sources) behave like the constant. Function-scope `ln`/`j`, reusing `line`/`i` for the final loop (175) and function-scope `x`/`y` did not help either.
- **`func_020508f4` (8).** `tdv2.py` (259 placements), block-scoped `n`, `i`, `tag` and `c`, and reusing the `str` parameter instead of `s` (40) left the `fn`/`dst` swap.

## Scope moves on overlay near-misses (overlays under 0x500 bytes, round ovhi22)

No new matches. This round tested the round itcm5 finding (function-scope temporaries decide registers, declaration order decides stack slots) on 24 overlay near-misses with 2 to 15 diffs. Drafts, logs and tools are in `scratchpad/ovhi22/` (`d/<mod>_<addr>/base.*`, `scope*.out`, `tdv2_a.out`).

- **`t/scope.py src func mod`** builds every scope move for one function (or for an inline with `SCOPE_IN=<name>`) and scores each move alone, then pairs of the best `SCOPE_PAIRS` (default 24). `SCOPE_DRY=<dir>` writes the variants without compiling them. The moves are:
  - `hoist`: every block-scoped declaration of a name goes to an enclosing block, up to function scope. Initialisers become assignments in place. In `.c` files the assignment goes after the block's declarations.
  - `hoistgrp`: all block-scoped names of one type in the same blocks move together, plus every 3-name subset when a group has 4 to 6 names. This is the round itcm5 `cx, cy, cz` move.
  - `sink`: a declaration moves into the innermost block that holds all of its uses, or into any block in between.
  - `wrap`: a declaration's use spans (for example two loops that share an index) each get a new block with their own declaration. The tool tries each span alone and all spans together.
  - `merge`: two block-scoped names of one type whose line ranges do not overlap become one function-scope variable.
  - `uninit`: `T a = e;` becomes `T a;` plus `a = e;` after the declaration list.
  - `order`: each function-scope declaration moves to every other slot of the declaration list.
- **Sanity check:** on the pre-fix itcm5 draft (`itcm5/9f9c/g4.cpp`), `hoistgrp cx+cy+cz` takes the masked score from 197 to 153. That is the known lever, so the tool does find this kind of move.
- **Results on the overlay batch:** about 7,400 compiles across all runs. No move improved any of the 24 functions. Many moves changed nothing at all, which means MWCC gave the same code whatever the scope. In the drafts that use an inline (`021708ec` `Lo_FindMsg`, `021be5dc` `CalcDepth`, `02199180` `DistSq9180`), declaration order inside the inline does matter. Writing the same body by hand in the caller does not: `02199180` scores 10 for all 18 orders and scopes of `dz, dx, dy`.

It also found the known `021c4460` 10 to 9, which needs an extra `self->unk_a4 = n` store and so is not real.
